*Pass Criteria*: All randomized writes complete with OKAY status and the final subordinate memory image matches the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-218-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_write_security_gating_test]]
===== 2.18 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_write_security_gating_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP write security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* `dbg_disable_i.sep_otp_jtag2axi = 1` disables the SEP OTP bridge; deriving the bit from lifecycle policy is SEP-level verification
* When gated, the SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP TDR (IR=0x22) remains selected while Update-DR requests are suppressed: no AXI-Lite traffic appears on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o` and a preloaded RAM sentinel at the gated address stays unchanged, both while gated and after the disable is released (no delayed replay)
* Two assert/release passes of the same disable prove the gate is repeatable
* A legal write succeeds before gating and again after each release (clean re-enable)

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Baseline write (un-gated):
a. With all disables clear, issue one SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP write (IR=0x22, op=0b10) and verify op = 0b00 (OKAY) and the AXI-Lite subordinate captured it.
. Gate (per pass, twice):
a. Assert `dbg_disable_i.sep_otp_jtag2axi = 1`, settle four toggling TCK cycles, preload a RAM sentinel, load IR=0x22, and shift a write request; verify no transaction on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o` and the sentinel unchanged.
b. Clear the disable without reset; verify the sentinel is still unchanged (no delayed replay), then verify normal operation resumes.
. Final restore check:
a. With all disables clear, verify a legal write again completes with OKAY.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_gate_no_traffic_chk` |No AXI-Lite write appears on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o` while gated
|`sep_otp_axi_gate_sentinel_chk` |The preloaded RAM sentinel is unchanged while gated and after release (no delayed replay)
|`sep_otp_axi_gate_restore_chk` |A legal write completes with OKAY after each release
|===

*Evidence*: cocotb: `CHK-AXI-COMPLETION`, `CHK-AXI-NOACT`, `CHK-AXI-NONVAC`, `CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-STRB`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` required and finalized by the shared AXI checker; SV-UVM adds `CHK-AXI-GATE-AR`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-AW`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-EXACT`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-W`, `CHK-AXI-WADDR`, `CHK-AXI-WDATA` and the `jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status`, and `ir_decode` scoreboard features. The scoreboard's blocked window (`CHK-AXI-BLOCKED`) fails on any monitored transaction across the gated attempt and its release, and the sentinel read-back is a plain assertion. The blocked path's positive control is the enabled baseline access of the same scenario.

*Pass Criteria*: With `dbg_disable_i.sep_otp_jtag2axi` asserted the write is suppressed with no AXI-Lite traffic and no memory mutation, and each release restores a passing OKAY write, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-3-jtag2axi-read-scenarios]]
==== 3. JTAG2AXI Read Scenarios

[[sec-31-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_read_test]]
===== 3.1 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_read_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric write-read path

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC fabric AXI4 single-operation write followed by a read-back of the same address
* SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) DR field packing: address (56-bit), data, wstrb, size, op
* Read op encoding `op=0b01` with data=0 and wstrb=0, and busy/terminal status polling
* Data integrity between the written word and the value returned by `OcahAxiSlaveAgent`

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SMC_JTAG2AXI_CAPS (IR=0x27) and read the 14-bit CAPS TDR.
a. Verify bus_type = AXI4, addr_size = 56, data_size = 64.
. Issue a single write through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge to a chosen target address.
a. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28).
b. Shift in target address, write data, wstrb=all-ones, size, op=0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
c. Poll via Capture-DR/Shift-DR until op status leaves Busy (0b11); verify op=0b00 (OKAY).
. Read back the same address through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
a. Reload SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28).
b. Shift in target address, data=0, wstrb=0, size, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
c. Poll op status until not Busy (0b11), then read returned data and status from the DR.
. Compare the returned read word against the previously written value and the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` reference.
. Verify the terminal op status is 0b00 (OKAY) on both the write and the read.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_single_wr_rd_status_chk` |Write and read both reach op=0b00 (OKAY) after leaving Busy
|`smc_axi_single_wr_rd_rdata_chk` |Returned read word equals the written value and the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` model
|`smc_axi_single_wr_rd_mem_chk` |`OcahAxiSlaveAgent` content at the target address matches the issued write
|===

*Evidence*: cocotb: `CHK-AXI-COMPLETION`, `CHK-AXI-RDATA`, `CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-STRB`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` required and finalized by the shared AXI checker; SV-UVM adds `CHK-AXI-ERR-INJ`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-AR`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-AW`, `CHK-AXI-RADDR`, `CHK-AXI-WADDR`, `CHK-AXI-WDATA` and the `jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status`, and `ir_decode` scoreboard features.

*Pass Criteria*: The write completes OKAY, the read-back returns the same word with OKAY status, and the memory side effect matches, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-32-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_test]]
===== 3.2 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric incrementing series write-read

*Test Coverage*:

* Sequential AXI4 accesses through the SMC fabric bridge across a block of incrementing addresses
* SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) preloading the start address and SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x2A) carrying each beat, the bridge advancing the series address by the beat size after every write
* Per-beat read data integrity against the data words written through the bridge
* OKAY terminal status in the SERIES_CTRL readback across the full series with no stuck-Busy condition
* Single-op addressing across many addresses is covered by <<sec-25-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_random_ops_test,2.5>> and <<sec-35-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_random_ops_test,3.5>>; every SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) shift carries its own address

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat-aligned base address, a stride equal to the 64-bit beat, and a beat count N; draw a distinct data word per beat.
. Write the series through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
a. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift the i-th data word through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x2A); Update-DR issues the write at base{plus}i*stride and advances the series address.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI write handshake and confirm `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` holds the data word at base{plus}i*stride.
. Read the series back in the same incrementing order.
a. For each beat i: load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with op=0b01 (Read), size, and address base{plus}i*stride; Update-DR.
b. Shift SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR once to launch the read and wait for the AXI read handshake; shift it again and take the Capture-DR payload as the returned beat.
. Compare every returned beat with the data word written to that address.
. Capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_series_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) after the write and read series
|`smc_axi_series_incr_rdata_chk` |Each returned beat matches the data word written to its address
|`smc_axi_series_incr_mem_chk` |`OcahAxiSlaveAgent` content over the block matches the issued write series
|===

*Evidence*: cocotb: `CHK-AXI-COMPLETION`, `CHK-AXI-RDATA`, `CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` required and finalized by the shared AXI checker; SV-UVM adds `CHK-AXI-NONVAC` and the `jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status`, and `ir_decode` scoreboard features.

*Pass Criteria*: Every incrementing-address write and read completes, the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY, and all per-beat comparisons match the written data, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-32a-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_narrow_test]]
===== 3.2a `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_narrow_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric 32-bit incrementing series write-read at beat offset +4

*Test Coverage*:

* A SERIES_DATA_INCR write then read stream with `size=2` whose base is beat-aligned plus four bytes
* Write-side AXI lane placement of the 32-bit logical payload onto the high half of the 64-bit beat
* Capture-DR returning that same logical payload extracted from the address-selected AXI RDATA lanes
* Per-beat memory compare at `base+i*4` against the written 32-bit pattern

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat-aligned base, add four, set stride to four bytes, and choose a beat count N.
. Write the series through SERIES_CTRL(WRITE) + SERIES_DATA_INCR and confirm each `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` location at `base+i*4` holds the 32-bit payload.
. Read the series back in the same order through SERIES_CTRL(READ) + SERIES_DATA_INCR; Capture-DR returns the logical 32-bit payload.
. Compare every returned beat with the written pattern and the responder memory.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_series_incr_narrow_status_chk` |Every op in the series reaches OKAY after Busy clears
|`smc_axi_series_incr_narrow_rdata_chk` |Each Capture-DR beat matches the written 32-bit payload
|`smc_axi_series_incr_narrow_mem_chk` |`OcahAxiSlaveAgent` content at each size-aligned address matches the issued write
|===

*Evidence*: cocotb: `CHK-AXI-COMPLETION`, `CHK-AXI-RDATA`, `CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` required and finalized by the shared AXI checker; SV-UVM adds `CHK-AXI-NONVAC` and the `jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status`, and `ir_decode` scoreboard features.

*Pass Criteria*: Every 32-bit incrementing write and read completes OKAY, Capture-DR returns the logical payload from the address-selected lanes, and all per-beat comparisons match, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-33-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_no_incr_test]]
===== 3.3 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_no_incr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric fixed-address series write-read

*Test Coverage*:

* Repeated AXI4 writes and reads through the SMC fabric bridge targeting one fixed address (no address advance)
* SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) holding one address while SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x2B) carries every beat without advancing it
* Returned data stability when the last-written value is read multiple times
* Series-length robustness with no DR/state corruption between repeated beats, and an unchanged address in the SERIES_CTRL readback

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a single beat-aligned target address and draw N distinct data words.
. Write the N words to the fixed address through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
a. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the target address; Update-DR.
b. For each word: shift it through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x2B); Update-DR issues the write and leaves the series address unchanged.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI write handshake.
. Read the fixed address N times through the bridge.
a. For each iteration: load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with op=0b01 (Read), size, and the target address; Update-DR.
b. Shift SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR once to launch the read and wait for the AXI read handshake; shift it again and take the Capture-DR payload as the returned beat.
. Verify every returned beat equals the last word written.
. Capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = the target address.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_series_no_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) at the unchanged address after the series
|`smc_axi_series_no_incr_rdata_chk` |Every returned beat equals the last word written
|`smc_axi_series_no_incr_mem_chk` |`OcahAxiSlaveAgent` at the fixed address holds the last word written across the series
|===

*Evidence*: cocotb: `CHK-AXI-COMPLETION`, `CHK-AXI-RDATA`, `CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` required and finalized by the shared AXI checker; SV-UVM adds `CHK-AXI-NONVAC` and the `jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status`, and `ir_decode` scoreboard features.

*Pass Criteria*: All fixed-address beats complete, every returned beat equals the last word written, and the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY at the unchanged address, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-34-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_with_error_test]]
===== 3.4 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_with_error_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric write-read with error status

*Test Coverage*:

* AXI4 write then read series through the SMC fabric bridge carried by SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x2C), the series-data form whose shift selects the address increment per beat and returns the previous beat's error status
* The increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1 on both legs: the second beat re-writes the held address, so the series spans three addresses and the later word wins at the held one
* A clean write leg with status bit 0 on every shift, then a read leg from one SERIES_CTRL preload in which one beat, chosen at random among the beats that first touch their address, returns SLVERR or DECERR
* Read-side Capture-DR returning the previous read's word and status bit: the bit is 1 only on the shift after the faulting read, every other beat returns the last word written to its address, and a held-address re-read after a faulting first read returns the word
* SERIES_CTRL readback reporting the pattern's final address after each leg, then a legal SINGLE_OP read outside the stream completing OKAY
* The error-series entries <<sec-47-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test,4.7>> and <<sec-48-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_read_with_status_test,4.8>> cover the same series-data form on incrementing-only streams together with the SERIES_CTRL fault code

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat-aligned base address whose four-slot footprint fits the target window and draw a data word per beat of the increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1.
. Write the series through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
a. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat: shift the data word and the beat's increment flag through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x2C); Update-DR issues the write and advances the series address only when the flag is set.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI write handshake, verify the returned status bit is 0, and confirm the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate holds the data word at the beat's address; shift one trailing word (data 0, increment 0) and verify its status bit is 0.
d. Capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify address = base{plus}3*stride.
. Pick the fault beat and its response (SLVERR or DECERR) from the scenario RNG and arm the responder to return that response once at the beat's address.
. Read the series back from one preload:
a. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with op=0b01 (Read), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. Shift four words carrying the beats' increment flags, then one trailing word (increment 0), through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS; every Update-DR launches a read, and shift k returns the word and status bit of read k-1.
c. Verify the status bit is 1 only on the shift after the fault beat and every non-faulting beat returns the last word written to its address.
d. Capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify address = base{plus}3*stride.
. Issue a legal SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) read of a preloaded slot outside the stream; verify OKAY and the word, proving recovery.
. Negative validation: `DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE=1` (cocotb) or `+DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE` (SV-UVM) keeps the expectation but leaves the fault unarmed, so the fault beat's checks must fail the run.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_series_status_bit_chk` |`CHK-J2A-STATUS-BIT`: status bit 0 on every write-leg shift and 1 only on the read-leg shift after the fault beat
|`smc_axi_series_status_rdata_chk` |Each non-faulting read beat returns the last word written to its address
|`smc_axi_series_status_addr_chk` |SERIES_CTRL address readback equals base {plus} 3*stride after each leg (the held beat does not advance the address)
|`smc_axi_series_status_recover_chk` |A legal single read outside the stream returns its word with OKAY; the armed response reaches the bus and is consumed (`CHK-AXI-RESP-EXPECTED` cocotb, `CHK-AXI-ERR-INJ` SV-UVM)
|===

*Evidence*: cocotb: `CHK-AXI-COMPLETION`, `CHK-AXI-CREDITS`, `CHK-AXI-NONVAC`, `CHK-AXI-RDATA`, `CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-RESP-EXPECTED`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` required and finalized by the shared AXI checker, with `CHK-J2A-STATUS-BIT` recorded by the sequence into the same finalization; SV-UVM adds `CHK-AXI-ERR-INJ` and the `jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status`, and `ir_decode` scoreboard features.

*Pass Criteria*: The write leg completes clean, the read leg's fault surfaces on the following shift's status bit and nowhere else, every other beat returns its word, both SERIES_CTRL readbacks show base{plus}3*stride, and the recovery read completes OKAY, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-35-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_random_ops_test]]
===== 3.5 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_random_ops_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric randomized reads

*Test Coverage*:

* Constrained-random SMC fabric AXI4 read addresses and sizes against a randomized reference
* SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) field packing under randomized address/size stimulus
* Per-op data integrity across a randomized address space
* Status correctness (OKAY) for all in-range randomized reads

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Preload `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` with a randomized reference image over the SMC fabric read window.
. Generate a constrained-random sequence of in-range read addresses (and legal sizes).
. Issue each randomized read through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
a. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28); shift in the random address, data=0, wstrb=0, size, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
b. Poll op status until not Busy (0b11); capture returned data and status.
. Compare each returned word with the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` reference model at that address.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_random_status_chk` |Every randomized read reaches op=0b00 (OKAY) after Busy clears
|`smc_axi_random_rdata_chk` |Each returned word matches the randomized `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` reference
|===

*Evidence*: cocotb: `CHK-AXI-COMPLETION`, `CHK-AXI-RDATA`, `CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` required and finalized by the shared AXI checker; SV-UVM adds `CHK-AXI-NONVAC`, `CHK-AXI-RADDR` and the `jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status`, and `ir_decode` scoreboard features.

*Pass Criteria*: All randomized reads complete OKAY and every returned word matches the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-36-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_security_gating_test]]
===== 3.6 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_security_gating_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric read security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* `dbg_disable_i.smc_jtag2axi = 1` gates the SMC fabric read bridge
* Gated read requests are suppressed: the TDR remains selected while no AXI traffic appears on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o`, and the request-activity counters hold flat across a blocked window that stays open through the disable's release (no delayed replay)
* Normal read operation restored after the disable clears, without any reset

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish a baseline SMC fabric read returning OKAY with expected data from `OcahAxiSlaveAgent`.
. Gate (per pass, twice): assert `dbg_disable_i.smc_jtag2axi = 1`, settle four toggling TCK cycles, load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28), and shift a read request.
a. Snapshot the AW/W/AR activity counters before the attempt and verify no AXI transaction issues on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o` across the gated window.
. Clear the disable without reset; hold the blocked window across the release and verify no queued request replays, then verify a normal read is restored with an exact activity delta (only the sanctioned restore read).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_rd_gate_noaxi_chk` |No AXI transaction on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o` while gated
|`smc_rd_gate_noreplay_chk` |The blocked window stays clean across the disable's release
|`smc_rd_gate_restore_chk` |Normal read with OKAY status resumes after the bit clears
|===

*Evidence*: cocotb: `CHK-AXI-COMPLETION`, `CHK-AXI-NOACT`, `CHK-AXI-NONVAC`, `CHK-AXI-RDATA`, `CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` required and finalized by the shared AXI checker; SV-UVM adds `CHK-AXI-GATE-AR`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-AW`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-EXACT`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-W` and the `jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status`, and `ir_decode` scoreboard features. The scoreboard's blocked window (`CHK-AXI-BLOCKED`) fails on any monitored transaction across the gated attempt and its release. The blocked path's positive control is the enabled baseline access of the same scenario.

*Pass Criteria*: The asserted disable suppresses all AXI activity with no delayed replay after release, and reads resume correctly once the disable clears, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-36a-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_security_gating_no_axi_activity_test]]
===== 3.6a `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_security_gating_no_axi_activity_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric read security gating with explicit no-AXI-activity check

*Test Coverage*:

* `dbg_disable_i.smc_jtag2axi = 1` prevents any SMC fabric read request from reaching AXI (deriving it from the lifecycle enables is SEP-level, <<dtp-vplan-scope>>)
* No `AWVALID` or `ARVALID` activity occurs while the bridge is gated on both passes
* Normal read activity resumes after the disable clears

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish a baseline SMC fabric read and confirm AXI activity is observed.
. Assert `dbg_disable_i.smc_jtag2axi = 1`, settle four toggling TCK cycles, and attempt a SMC fabric read; repeat the gate on a second pass.
. Monitor AXI request channels for a bounded window and verify no AW/AR valid activity occurs.
. Clear the disable without reset and verify a normal read again produces AXI activity and OKAY status.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_rd_gate_no_aw_ar_chk` |No AWVALID or ARVALID while the bridge is gated
|`smc_rd_gate_restore_activity_chk` |AXI read activity resumes after gating clears
|===

*Evidence*: cocotb: `CHK-AXI-NOACT`, `CHK-AXI-NONVAC`, `CHK-AXI-RDATA`, `CHK-AXI-RESP` required and finalized by the shared AXI checker; SV-UVM adds `CHK-AXI-GATE-AR`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-AW`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-EXACT`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-W` and the `jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status`, and `ir_decode` scoreboard features. The scoreboard's blocked window (`CHK-AXI-BLOCKED`) fails on any monitored transaction across the gated attempt and its release. The blocked path's positive control is the enabled baseline access of the same scenario.

*Pass Criteria*: The asserted disable blocks all SMC fabric AXI read activity on both passes and normal reads resume after each release, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-37-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_single_write_read_test]]
===== 3.7 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_single_write_read_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP write-read path

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC OTP AXI4-Lite single-operation write followed by a read-back of the same address
* SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) DR field packing: 32-bit address, 32-bit data, wstrb, op
* CAPS reporting bus_type = AXI4-Lite, addr_size = 32, data_size = 32
* Data integrity between the written word and the value returned by the AXI-Lite subordinate

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SMC_OTP_JTAG2AXI_CAPS (IR=0x1B) and read the 14-bit CAPS TDR.
a. Verify bus_type = AXI4-Lite (CAPS bit 0 = 1), addr_size = 32, data_size = 32.
. Issue a single write through the SMC OTP AXI-Lite bridge to a chosen 32-bit address.
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C).
b. Shift in target address, write data, wstrb=all-ones, op=0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
c. Poll until op status leaves Busy (0b11); verify op=0b00 (OKAY).
. Read back the same address through the SMC OTP AXI-Lite bridge.
a. Reload SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C).
b. Shift in target address, data=0, wstrb=0, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
c. Poll op status until not Busy; read returned data and status from the DR.
. Compare the returned read word against the written value and the AXI-Lite subordinate model.
. Verify the terminal op status is 0b00 (OKAY) on both the write and the read.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_single_wr_rd_status_chk` |Write and read both reach op=0b00 (OKAY) after leaving Busy
|`smc_otp_single_wr_rd_rdata_chk` |Returned read word equals the written value and the AXI-Lite model
|`smc_otp_single_wr_rd_mem_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate content at the target address matches the issued write
|===

*Evidence*: cocotb: `CHK-AXI-COMPLETION`, `CHK-AXI-RDATA`, `CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-STRB`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` required and finalized by the shared AXI checker; SV-UVM adds `CHK-AXI-ERR-INJ`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-AR`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-AW`, `CHK-AXI-RADDR`, `CHK-AXI-WADDR`, `CHK-AXI-WDATA` and the `jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status`, and `ir_decode` scoreboard features.

*Pass Criteria*: The write completes OKAY, the read-back returns the same word with OKAY status, and the memory side effect matches, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-38-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_test]]
===== 3.8 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP incrementing series write-read

*Test Coverage*:

* Sequential AXI4-Lite accesses through the SMC OTP bridge across a block of incrementing 32-bit addresses
* SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) preloading the start address and SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x1E) carrying each beat, the bridge advancing the series address by the beat size after every write
* Per-beat read data integrity against the data words written through the bridge
* OKAY terminal status in the SERIES_CTRL readback across the full series with no stuck-Busy condition
* Single-op addressing across many addresses is covered by <<sec-211-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_random_ops_test,2.11>> and <<sec-311-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_read_random_ops_test,3.11>>; every SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) shift carries its own address

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat-aligned base address, a stride equal to the 32-bit beat, and a beat count N; draw a distinct data word per beat.
. Write the series through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge.
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift the i-th data word through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x1E); Update-DR issues the write at base{plus}i*stride and advances the series address.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write handshake.
. Read the series back in the same incrementing order.
a. For each beat i: load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with op=0b01 (Read), size, and address base{plus}i*stride; Update-DR.
b. Shift SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR once to launch the read and wait for the AXI-Lite read handshake; shift it again and take the Capture-DR payload as the returned beat.
. Compare every returned beat with the data word written to that address.
. Capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_series_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) after the write and read series
|`smc_otp_series_incr_rdata_chk` |Each returned beat matches the data word written to its address
|`smc_otp_series_incr_mem_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate content over the block matches the issued write series
|===

*Evidence*: cocotb: `CHK-AXI-COMPLETION`, `CHK-AXI-RDATA`, `CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` required and finalized by the shared AXI checker; SV-UVM adds `CHK-AXI-NONVAC` and the `jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status`, and `ir_decode` scoreboard features.

*Pass Criteria*: Every incrementing-address write and read completes, the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY, and all per-beat comparisons match the written data, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-39-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_no_incr_test]]
===== 3.9 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_no_incr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP fixed-address series write-read

*Test Coverage*:

* Repeated AXI4-Lite writes and reads through the SMC OTP bridge targeting one fixed 32-bit address (no address advance)
* SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) holding one address while SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x1F) carries every beat without advancing it
* Returned data stability when the last-written value is read multiple times
* Series-length robustness with no DR/state corruption between repeated beats, and an unchanged address in the SERIES_CTRL readback

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a single beat-aligned target address and draw N distinct data words.
. Write the N words to the fixed address through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge.
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the target address; Update-DR.
b. For each word: shift it through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x1F); Update-DR issues the write and leaves the series address unchanged.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write handshake.
. Read the fixed address N times through the bridge.
a. For each iteration: load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with op=0b01 (Read), size, and the target address; Update-DR.
b. Shift SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR once to launch the read and wait for the AXI-Lite read handshake; shift it again and take the Capture-DR payload as the returned beat.
. Verify every returned beat equals the last word written.
. Capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = the target address.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_series_no_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) at the unchanged address after the series
|`smc_otp_series_no_incr_rdata_chk` |Every returned beat equals the last word written
|`smc_otp_series_no_incr_mem_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate at the fixed address holds the last word written across the series
|===

*Evidence*: cocotb: `CHK-AXI-COMPLETION`, `CHK-AXI-RDATA`, `CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` required and finalized by the shared AXI checker; SV-UVM adds `CHK-AXI-NONVAC` and the `jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status`, and `ir_decode` scoreboard features.

*Pass Criteria*: All fixed-address beats complete, every returned beat equals the last word written, and the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY at the unchanged address, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-310-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_with_error_test]]
===== 3.10 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_with_error_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP write-read with error status

*Test Coverage*:

* AXI4-Lite write then read series through the SMC OTP bridge carried by SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x20), the series-data form whose shift selects the address increment per beat and returns the previous beat's error status
* The increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1 on both legs: the second beat re-writes the held address, so the series spans three addresses and the later word wins at the held one
* A clean write leg with status bit 0 on every shift, then a read leg from one SERIES_CTRL preload in which one beat, chosen at random among the beats that first touch their address, returns SLVERR or DECERR
* Read-side Capture-DR returning the previous read's word and status bit: the bit is 1 only on the shift after the faulting read, every other beat returns the last word written to its address, and a held-address re-read after a faulting first read returns the word
* SERIES_CTRL readback reporting the pattern's final address after each leg, then a legal SINGLE_OP read outside the stream completing OKAY
* The error-series entries <<sec-416-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test,4.16>> and <<sec-417-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_incr_read_with_status_test,4.17>> cover the same series-data form on incrementing-only streams together with the SERIES_CTRL fault code

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat-aligned base address whose four-slot footprint fits the target window and draw a data word per beat of the increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1.
. Write the series through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge.
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat: shift the data word and the beat's increment flag through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x20); Update-DR issues the write and advances the series address only when the flag is set.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write handshake, verify the returned status bit is 0, and confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate holds the data word at the beat's address; shift one trailing word (data 0, increment 0) and verify its status bit is 0.
d. Capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify address = base{plus}3*stride.
. Pick the fault beat and its response (SLVERR or DECERR) from the scenario RNG and arm the responder to return that response once at the beat's address.
. Read the series back from one preload:
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with op=0b01 (Read), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. Shift four words carrying the beats' increment flags, then one trailing word (increment 0), through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS; every Update-DR launches a read, and shift k returns the word and status bit of read k-1.
c. Verify the status bit is 1 only on the shift after the fault beat and every non-faulting beat returns the last word written to its address.
d. Capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify address = base{plus}3*stride.
. Issue a legal SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) read of a preloaded slot outside the stream; verify OKAY and the word, proving recovery.
. Negative validation: `DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE=1` (cocotb) or `+DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE` (SV-UVM) keeps the expectation but leaves the fault unarmed, so the fault beat's checks must fail the run.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_series_status_bit_chk` |`CHK-J2A-STATUS-BIT`: status bit 0 on every write-leg shift and 1 only on the read-leg shift after the fault beat
|`smc_otp_series_status_rdata_chk` |Each non-faulting read beat returns the last word written to its address
|`smc_otp_series_status_addr_chk` |SERIES_CTRL address readback equals base {plus} 3*stride after each leg (the held beat does not advance the address)
|`smc_otp_series_status_recover_chk` |A legal single read outside the stream returns its word with OKAY; the armed response reaches the bus and is consumed (`CHK-AXI-RESP-EXPECTED` cocotb, `CHK-AXI-ERR-INJ` SV-UVM)
|===

*Evidence*: cocotb: `CHK-AXI-COMPLETION`, `CHK-AXI-CREDITS`, `CHK-AXI-NONVAC`, `CHK-AXI-RDATA`, `CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-RESP-EXPECTED`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` required and finalized by the shared AXI checker, with `CHK-J2A-STATUS-BIT` recorded by the sequence into the same finalization; SV-UVM adds `CHK-AXI-ERR-INJ` and the `jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status`, and `ir_decode` scoreboard features.

*Pass Criteria*: The write leg completes clean, the read leg's fault surfaces on the following shift's status bit and nowhere else, every other beat returns its word, both SERIES_CTRL readbacks show base{plus}3*stride, and the recovery read completes OKAY, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-311-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_read_random_ops_test]]
===== 3.11 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_read_random_ops_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP randomized reads

*Test Coverage*:

* Constrained-random SMC OTP AXI4-Lite read addresses against a randomized 32-bit reference
* SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) field packing under randomized address stimulus
* Per-op data integrity across a randomized address space
* Status correctness (OKAY) for all in-range randomized reads

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Preload the SMC OTP AXI-Lite subordinate with a randomized reference image over the read window.
. Generate a constrained-random sequence of in-range 32-bit read addresses.
. Issue each randomized read through the SMC OTP AXI-Lite bridge.
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C); shift in the random address, data=0, wstrb=0, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
b. Poll op status until not Busy (0b11); capture returned data and status.
. Compare each returned word with the AXI-Lite subordinate reference model at that address.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_random_status_chk` |Every randomized read reaches op=0b00 (OKAY) after Busy clears
|`smc_otp_random_rdata_chk` |Each returned word matches the randomized AXI-Lite reference
|===

*Evidence*: cocotb: `CHK-AXI-COMPLETION`, `CHK-AXI-RDATA`, `CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` required and finalized by the shared AXI checker; SV-UVM adds `CHK-AXI-NONVAC`, `CHK-AXI-RADDR` and the `jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status`, and `ir_decode` scoreboard features.

*Pass Criteria*: All randomized reads complete OKAY and every returned word matches the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-312-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_read_security_gating_test]]
===== 3.12 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_read_security_gating_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP read security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* `dbg_disable_i.smc_otp_jtag2axi = 1` gates the SMC OTP read bridge
* Gated read requests are suppressed: the TDR remains selected while no AXI-Lite traffic appears on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`, with no delayed replay after the disable is released
* Normal read operation restored after the disable clears, without any reset

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish a baseline SMC OTP read returning OKAY with expected data from the AXI-Lite subordinate.
. Gate (per pass, twice): assert `dbg_disable_i.smc_otp_jtag2axi = 1`, settle four toggling TCK cycles, load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C), and shift a read request.
a. Verify no AXI-Lite transaction issues on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o` across the gated window.
. Clear the disable without reset; verify no queued request replays, then verify normal reads resume.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_rd_gate_noaxi_chk` |No AXI-Lite transaction on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o` while gated
|`smc_otp_rd_gate_noreplay_chk` |No queued request replays after the disable's release
|`smc_otp_rd_gate_restore_chk` |Normal read with OKAY status resumes after the disable clears
|===

*Evidence*: cocotb: `CHK-AXI-COMPLETION`, `CHK-AXI-NOACT`, `CHK-AXI-NONVAC`, `CHK-AXI-RDATA`, `CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` required and finalized by the shared AXI checker; SV-UVM adds `CHK-AXI-GATE-AR`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-AW`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-EXACT`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-W` and the `jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status`, and `ir_decode` scoreboard features. The scoreboard's blocked window (`CHK-AXI-BLOCKED`) fails on any monitored transaction across the gated attempt and its release. The blocked path's positive control is the enabled baseline access of the same scenario.

*Pass Criteria*: The asserted `dbg_disable_i.smc_otp_jtag2axi` suppresses all AXI-Lite activity with no delayed replay after release, and reads resume correctly once the disable clears, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-313-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_single_write_read_test]]
===== 3.13 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_single_write_read_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP write-read path

*Test Coverage*:

* SEP OTP AXI4-Lite single-operation write followed by a read-back of the same address
* SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22) DR field packing: 32-bit address, 32-bit data, wstrb, op
* CAPS reporting bus_type = AXI4-Lite, addr_size = 32, data_size = 32
* Data integrity between the written word and the value returned by the AXI-Lite subordinate

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SEP_OTP_JTAG2AXI_CAPS (IR=0x21) and read the 14-bit CAPS TDR.
a. Verify bus_type = AXI4-Lite (CAPS bit 0 = 1), addr_size = 32, data_size = 32.
. Issue a single write through the SEP OTP AXI-Lite bridge to a chosen 32-bit address.
a. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22).
b. Shift in target address, write data, wstrb=all-ones, op=0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
c. Poll until op status leaves Busy (0b11); verify op=0b00 (OKAY).
. Read back the same address through the SEP OTP AXI-Lite bridge.
a. Reload SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22).
b. Shift in target address, data=0, wstrb=0, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
c. Poll op status until not Busy; read returned data and status from the DR.
. Compare the returned read word against the written value and the AXI-Lite subordinate model.
. Verify the terminal op status is 0b00 (OKAY) on both the write and the read.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_single_wr_rd_status_chk` |Write and read both reach op=0b00 (OKAY) after leaving Busy
|`sep_otp_single_wr_rd_rdata_chk` |Returned read word equals the written value and the AXI-Lite model
|`sep_otp_single_wr_rd_mem_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate content at the target address matches the issued write
|===

*Evidence*: cocotb: `CHK-AXI-COMPLETION`, `CHK-AXI-RDATA`, `CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-STRB`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` required and finalized by the shared AXI checker; SV-UVM adds `CHK-AXI-NONVAC`, `CHK-AXI-RADDR`, `CHK-AXI-WADDR`, `CHK-AXI-WDATA` and the `jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status`, and `ir_decode` scoreboard features.

*Pass Criteria*: The write completes OKAY, the read-back returns the same word with OKAY status, and the memory side effect matches, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-314-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_test]]
