[[sec-59-dtp_3dcr_stap_sel_sep_test]]
===== 5.9 `dtp_3dcr_stap_sel_sep_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (primary TAP master and the downstream slave TAPs behind every STAP host port), OCAH-local STAP chain model

*Objective*: SEP STAP selection proved end to end against a downstream TAP

*Test Coverage*:

* TAP_3DCR (IR=0x0E) selects the SEP debug STAP by writing 3DCR `stap_sel=1`, `tms_hold=1`, `config_hold=1` through composed full-chain scans (the `TAP_3DCR` data register is the 2-bit PTAP 3DCR followed serially by the STAP SIB chain); every scan issued while a STAP is selected is composed over the full network, IR scans included, because the PTAP routes its instruction shift-out into the STAP chain
* A behavioral downstream TAP (`ocah_jtag_vip` slave device: 5-bit IR, distinct IDCODE, one writable `DS_TDR` of a distinct width) is spliced behind every STAP host port; its IR and selected data register become chain segments the reference model predicts independently of the device engine
* While selected, the downstream IDCODE reads back through the STAP, a network-wide IR scan selects `DS_TDR`, and a per-pass random value written through the chain reads back and is confirmed by the device's Update-DR record; the SEP host port forwards (`tdo_oen` pulses, `tms` follows the live TMS) as corroboration
* `dbg_disable_i.stap_sep = 1` stops forwarding, parks the host `tms` at the stored `tms_hold=1` so the downstream TAP sits in Test-Logic-Reset, blocks a 3DCR update attempted while gated, and leaves the downstream register unchanged with no Update-DR recorded
* Once the disable clears, without any reset, the stored selection resumes: the downstream answers IDCODE again (park reset proven) and reloading `DS_TDR` returns the pre-gate value; an unrelated STAP's downstream TAP completes a full load/write/read cycle while the target stays gated and records no update; a fresh configuration recovers fully

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Flush the TAP_3DCR configuration chain with one over-length all-zero scan, then configure the SEP debug STAP with composed scans: open its SIB, then write its 3DCR `stap_sel=1, tms_hold=1, config_hold=1` (PTAP 3DCR held at `select=1, config_hold=1`).
. Run a state-maintaining chain scan inside a temporal window; verify the host `tdo_oen` pulses, host `tms` follows the live TMS, and the captured chain matches the reference model.
. Read the downstream IDCODE through the chain (`CHK-DS-IDCODE`); select `DS_TDR` with a composed IR scan; write a per-pass random value; verify the device recorded exactly one Update-DR with that value (`CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE-COUNT`); read it back through the chain (`CHK-DS-TDR-READBACK`).
. Assert `dbg_disable_i.stap_sep = 1` and attempt a randomized deselecting 3DCR write:
a. Verify `tdo_oen` never pulses and `tms` parks at the stored `tms_hold=1` across the whole gated window.
b. Verify the chain readback shows the update was ignored (the STAP captures its masked `stap_sel` as 0 while the stored payload survives).
c. Verify the downstream device recorded no Update-DR, still holds the written value (`CHK-DS-TDR-HOLD`), and sits in Test-Logic-Reset (`CHK-DS-PARKED-TLR`).
. Clear the disable without reset; verify forwarding resumes from the stored selection, the downstream answers IDCODE again, and reloading `DS_TDR` reads back the pre-gate value (`CHK-DS-TDR-RESUME`).
. Re-assert the disable and configure a neighbor STAP; verify the neighbor forwards while the gated port stays quiet, the neighbor's downstream TAP completes a load/write/read cycle, and the gated port's device records no update (isolation).
. Clear all disables, flush (composed IR load parking the spliced downstream TAPs on BYPASS), and configure fresh; verify full recovery with a new `DS_TDR` write and readback.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`stap_sep_select_chk` |Selected SEP debug STAP forwards: `tdo_oen` pulses and `tms` follows the live TMS (`CHK-SCAN-WIN`)
|`stap_sep_readback_chk` |Composed chain readback matches the reference model in every state (`CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`)
|`stap_sep_downstream_chk` |Downstream IDCODE and `DS_TDR` read back through the selected STAP; the device's Update-DR record matches the write (`CHK-DS-IDCODE`, `CHK-DS-TDR-READBACK`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE-COUNT`)
|`stap_sep_gate_chk` |With `dbg_disable_i.stap_sep` asserted, forwarding stops, a gated 3DCR update is ignored, and the downstream register is frozen in Test-Logic-Reset (`CHK-DS-TDR-HOLD`, `CHK-DS-PARKED-TLR`)
|`stap_sep_isolation_chk` |An unrelated STAP and its downstream TAP stay usable while the SEP debug STAP is gated
|`stap_sep_recover_chk` |Selection resumes without reset after release with the pre-gate downstream value intact (`CHK-DS-TDR-RESUME`), and a fresh configuration fully recovers
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-SCAN-WIN` (the SEP host port forwarding while selected, quiet and parked while gated, the neighbor forwarding while the SEP port stays gated), `CHK-SCAN-CHAIN` (every composed chain readback against the STAP model), `CHK-DS-IDCODE`, `CHK-DS-TDR-READBACK`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE-COUNT`, `CHK-DS-TDR-HOLD`, `CHK-DS-PARKED-TLR`, `CHK-DS-TDR-RESUME` (the downstream TAP behind the SEP port and behind the neighbor), `CHK-TAP-RESET-TLR` required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds `CHK-SCAN-IR-LEN` and `CHK-SCAN-DR-LEN` from the env checker, the `ir_decode` scoreboard feature, and `CHK-TAP-STATE`.

*Pass Criteria*: The SEP debug STAP forwards only while selected and enabled, the downstream TAP behind it is programmable and readable exactly then, `dbg_disable_i.stap_sep` alone gates it with downstream state preserved and no delayed effect, and recovery needs no reset, with every required `CHK-*` ID recorded and zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-510-dtp_ext_stap_scan_test]]
===== 5.10 `dtp_ext_stap_scan_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local STAP model

*Objective*: Extended STAP scan

*Test Coverage*:

* Extended STAP scan interface (`+jtag_stap_host_scan_*+`) enabled via PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=1
* `jtag_stap_host_scan_ctrl_o` asserts scan control during DR operations; the host scan-in is the loopback of the host scan-out in `tb_top`, so the bench observes the control path and no independent data path
* Clearing PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=0 changes the data routing only; the host scan controls stay active during PTAP scan activity; a scan with SELECT=0 is judged for its pulsing host shift strobe
* `dbg_disable_i.stap_host = 1` forces all four `jtag_stap_host_scan_ctrl_o` signals quiet across the whole gated window (temporal-window evidence) and bypasses scan-in (PTAP receives the `stap_host_scan_out` loopback instead of external scan-in)
* Normal scan operation resumes, without any reset, after the disable clears

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Enable the extended STAP scan interface by writing PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=1 via `OcahJtagMasterDriver`.
. Inside a temporal window, verify `jtag_stap_host_scan_ctrl_o` select and shift-enable pulse during DR operations; shift a known pattern out on `jtag_stap_host_scan_out_o` and sample the host `select` level after the shift.
. Clear PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=0 and shift a DR scan under a temporal window: the host shift strobe still pulses (the strobes follow every PTAP scan) and the sampled host select level is recorded.
. Security gating:
a. Re-enable scan (PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=1), then assert `dbg_disable_i.stap_host = 1`.
b. Inside a temporal window covering the whole gated DR operation, verify none of the four host scan-control signals pulses and scan-in is disconnected (PTAP sees the `stap_host_scan_out` loopback rather than external scan-in).
c. Clear the disable without reset and verify scan control and the data path are restored.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ext_stap_enable_chk` |The host scan controls pulse during DR operations when PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=1
|`ext_stap_deselect_chk` |With PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=0 the host shift strobe still pulses across a DR scan (`CHK-SCAN-WIN`); SELECT changes the data routing, not the strobes
|`ext_stap_gate_chk` |With `dbg_disable_i.stap_host` asserted, all four host scan controls stay quiet across the gated window and scan-in is bypassed
|`ext_stap_restore_chk` |Normal STAP host scan resumes, without reset, after the disable clears
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-SCAN-WIN` (the host `select` and `shift_en` active with the PTAP select set, the host `shift_en` active across the deselected scan, all four host controls quiet across the gated shift, active again after the release without reset), `CHK-TAP-RESET-TLR` required in both realizations; the sampled `jtag_stap_host_select` level after each shift is a plain assertion, and the bench compares no scan data on `jtag_stap_host_scan_in_i`, the loopback of the host scan-out (<<dtp-vplan-signoff-scan-dispositions>>); SV-UVM adds `CHK-SCAN-IR-LEN` and `CHK-SCAN-DR-LEN` from the env checker, the `ir_decode` scoreboard feature, and `CHK-TAP-STATE`.

*Pass Criteria*: The extended STAP scan controls pulse when selected, keep pulsing when deselected, are suppressed and loopback-bypassed while `dbg_disable_i.stap_host` is asserted, and recover without reset when it clears, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-511-dtp_3dcr_config_hold_test]]
===== 5.11 `dtp_3dcr_config_hold_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local STAP model

*Objective*: 3DCR config hold

*Test Coverage*:

* CONFIG_HOLD in both PTAP_3DCR and STAP 3DCR protects register contents across Test-Logic-Reset (TLR)
* With CONFIG_HOLD=1, SELECT (PTAP) and SELECT/TMS_HOLD (STAP) survive a forced TLR; TRST clears them regardless
* With CONFIG_HOLD=0, a TLR clears the affected 3DCR fields to 0
* Configuration values are exercised through composed TAP_3DCR chain scans (`OcahJtagMasterDriver`) against the STAP reference model, for every STAP in a seeded order, with a seeded STAP SELECT so the retained selection is proved spliced and unspliced

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Flush the chain; write PTAP_3DCR with CONFIG_HOLD=h, SELECT=1 and open the STAP's SIB; Update-DR.
. Write the STAP 3DCR with CONFIG_HOLD=h, a seeded SELECT, TMS_HOLD=1; Update-DR.
. Force TLR (5x TMS=1), or TRST for the TRST sub-case; the IR returns to IDCODE.
. Reload TAP_3DCR with a composed IR scan (every IR scan shifts through the STAP chain).
a. With the PTAP SELECT retained (h=1 across TLR), a maintain scan returns the chain: verify the captured PTAP SELECT=1 and CONFIG_HOLD=1.
b. With the PTAP SELECT cleared (h=0, or TRST), TDO carries the PTAP 3DCR itself: verify both bits read 0 and that a marker shifted behind them arrives two bits later (the chain return would delay it by the chain length).
. Re-select the PTAP and reopen the STAP's SIB; a maintain scan then carries the STAP 3DCR.
a. h=1 across TLR: verify SELECT and TMS_HOLD read back as written.
b. h=0 across TLR, or TRST: verify every STAP 3DCR field reads 0.
. Repeat the three sub-cases (h=1+TLR, h=0+TLR, h=1+TRST) in a seeded order for every STAP.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`config_hold_preserve_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: with CONFIG_HOLD=1 the maintain scans after TLR capture PTAP SELECT=1/CONFIG_HOLD=1 and the STAP's written SELECT/TMS_HOLD
|`config_hold_clear_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-OBS` and `CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: with CONFIG_HOLD=0 the PTAP 3DCR reads 0 over the TDR return path after TLR (marker position proves the path) and the reopened STAP 3DCR captures 0; TRST clears both regardless of CONFIG_HOLD
|`config_hold_data_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: every chain readback (PTAP 3DCR, SIB, STAP 3DCR fields) matches the STAP reference model's predicted capture after each reset
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-SCAN-CHAIN` (the PTAP and STAP 3DCR fields read back through the maintain scans after each Test-Logic-Reset and TRST case, for every STAP), `CHK-SCAN-OBS` (the cleared PTAP 3DCR over the TDR return path and the marker position that proves the path), `CHK-TAP-RESET-TLR` required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds `CHK-SCAN-IR-LEN`, `CHK-SCAN-DR-LEN`, and `CHK-TAP-TLR-TMS5` from the env checkers, the `ir_decode` scoreboard feature, and `CHK-TAP-STATE`.

*Pass Criteria*: CONFIG_HOLD=1 holds both 3DCRs across TLR while CONFIG_HOLD=0 allows TLR to clear them, matching the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-512-dtp_3dcr_tms_hold_test]]
===== 5.12 `dtp_3dcr_tms_hold_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local STAP model

*Objective*: 3DCR TMS hold

*Test Coverage*:

* TMS_HOLD field in the STAP 3DCR sets the parked TMS value driven to a deselected STAP
* TMS_HOLD=1 parks the deselected STAP TMS high (drives toward TLR); TMS_HOLD=0 parks it low (drives toward RTI)
* Parked value is observed on the STAP TAP control output (`jtag_stap_<x>_tms`) for a whole maintain scan while the STAP is deselected (SELECT=0); the deselected port never drives `tdo_oen`
* Both TMS_HOLD polarities are exercised for every STAP, in a seeded order, through composed TAP_3DCR chain scans (`OcahJtagMasterDriver`)

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Flush the chain, set the PTAP SELECT, and open the STAP's SIB; Update-DR.
. Select the STAP via its 3DCR (SELECT=1, TMS_HOLD=h); Update-DR.
. Under a temporal window over a maintain scan while the STAP is selected, count `jtag_stap_<x>_tms` and `jtag_stap_<x>_tdo_oen` high samples per TCK cycle: `tdo_oen` pulses and TMS follows the live TMS (mixed samples). This is the positive control for the parked checks that follow, on the same port and the same window shape.
. Deselect the STAP (SELECT=0, TMS_HOLD=h); Update-DR.
. Under a temporal window over a maintain scan, count `jtag_stap_<x>_tms` and `jtag_stap_<x>_tdo_oen` high samples per TCK cycle.
a. h=1: TMS is high on every cycle (parked high toward TLR).
b. h=0: TMS is never high (parked low toward RTI).
c. `tdo_oen` never pulses.
. Compare the maintain scan's captured chain (SIB, SELECT=0, TMS_HOLD=h) against the STAP reference model.
. Repeat for h in {1, 0} in a seeded order and for every STAP in a seeded order.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`tms_hold_selected_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN`: while the STAP is selected its `tdo_oen` pulses and its TMS follows the live TMS across the maintain scan (positive control)
|`tms_hold_high_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN`: with TMS_HOLD=1 the deselected STAP's TMS count equals the window's TCK count and `tdo_oen` stays quiet
|`tms_hold_low_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN`: with TMS_HOLD=0 the deselected STAP's TMS count is 0 and `tdo_oen` stays quiet
|`tms_hold_data_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: the maintain scan's captured 3DCR (SELECT=0, TMS_HOLD=h) matches the STAP reference model, which also supplies the expected parked polarity
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-SCAN-WIN` (the selected port's `tdo_oen` pulsing and TMS mixed, then the deselected port's TMS count equal to the window's TCK count with `tms_hold=1` and zero with `tms_hold=0` with `tdo_oen` quiet in both, every window with DUT shift cycles), `CHK-SCAN-CHAIN` (the maintain scans' captured 3DCR against the STAP model, for every STAP and both polarities), `CHK-TAP-RESET-TLR` required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds `CHK-SCAN-IR-LEN` and `CHK-SCAN-DR-LEN` from the env checker, the `ir_decode` scoreboard feature, and `CHK-TAP-STATE`.

*Pass Criteria*: A selected STAP forwards, and once deselected it parks its TMS at the configured TMS_HOLD value for both polarities, matching the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-513-dtp_scan_dbg_disable_matrix_test]]
===== 5.13 `dtp_scan_dbg_disable_matrix_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local iJTAG SIB and STAP chain models

*Objective*: Debug-disable matrix over the eight scan-side gate fields

*Test Coverage*:

* Deterministic one-hot rows for `stap_io`, `stap_smc`, `stap_sep`, `stap_extra`, `stap_host`, `dft_secure`, `dft_nonsecure`, and `dfd`, plus the all-clear and all-disabled boundary masks and seeded multi-hot masks
* Per row: each iJTAG SIB requested open follows its disable (temporal window: a gated SIB's four scan controls never pulse, an effective SIB's select is seen high); each STAP configured through composed TAP_3DCR scans follows its disable (a gated port never forwards and its gated 3DCR write is ignored, verified by chain readback); the extended host scan interface follows `stap_host`
* After the all-disabled row: releasing every disable without reset shows no gated open attempt stuck (release without replay), and a full all-clear row recovers
* Mixed rows prove unrelated-resource isolation; every checker-verified outcome samples its `dbg_disable_cg` cell; the test requires all sixteen scan-side cells and emits the JSON coverage artifact

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. For each row mask (all-clear, one-hot per field, seeded multi-hot, all-disabled last): drive the full disable vector and settle four toggling TCK cycles.
. iJTAG evidence: request all three SIBs open inside a temporal window and check each SIB's allowed/blocked outcome against the reference model.
. STAP evidence: flush the TAP_3DCR chain, open all four SIBs and write all four 3DCR payloads in composed scans under the row mask, then run a windowed maintain scan; check per-port forwarding vs quiet+parked outcomes, the host scan-control window, and the chain readback (gated payload writes ignored).
. After the final all-disabled row: clear all disables, observe without reprogramming, and verify no SIB select pulses (no delayed open); then rerun the all-clear row as recovery.
. Emit the `dbg_disable_cg` JSON artifact and require all sixteen scan-side cells.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`scan_matrix_ijtag_chk` |Each SIB's select/window outcome follows exactly its disable field
|`scan_matrix_stap_chk` |Each STAP's forwarding outcome follows exactly its disable field; gated 3DCR writes are ignored
|`scan_matrix_host_chk` |The extended host scan controls follow `stap_host`
|`scan_matrix_readback_chk` |Composed chain readbacks match the reference model under every mask
|`scan_matrix_noreplay_chk` |No gated open attempt sticks after the disables release
|`scan_matrix_recovery_chk` |The all-clear row fully recovers after the all-disabled row
|===

*Evidence*: cocotb: the sixteen scan-side ledger cells through `require_cells` and the JSON coverage artifact; the per-row window, forwarding, and chain comparisons are plain assertions (<<dtp-vplan-signoff-scan-dispositions>>). SV-UVM: `CHK-SCAN-WIN` (each SIB's and each host port's allowed or blocked outcome per row), `CHK-SCAN-CHAIN` (the composed chain readbacks under every mask), `CHK-SCAN-LEN`, `CHK-TAP-RESET-TLR` required; `CHK-SCAN-IR-LEN` and `CHK-SCAN-DR-LEN` from the env checker, the `ir_decode` scoreboard feature, and `CHK-TAP-STATE`.

*Pass Criteria*: Every row mask produces the per-field allowed/blocked outcomes with temporal-window evidence, no delayed effects, and full recovery, closing all sixteen scan-side `dbg_disable_cg` cells, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-6-xtrig-ctp-and-ctm-scenarios]]
==== 6. XTRIG, CTP, and CTM Scenarios

[[sec-61-dtp_xtrig_wire_or_test]]
===== 6.1 `dtp_xtrig_wire_or_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTP/CTM BFMs

*Objective*: CTP wire-OR

*Test Coverage*:

* CTP wire-OR mode (CONFIG.MODE=0) pulse synchronization with configurable pulse stretching
* Output pulse width equals (STRETCH_MULT{plus}1) clock cycles for a wide multiplier, the single-cycle minimum, and a seeded width in between
* External CT_Req_in input synchronized into the `clk_i` domain before delivery
* STATUS.BUSY reads 1 over the CSR path while a pulse of at least nine cycles (STRETCH_MULT >= 8, the CSR read latency bound) is active and 0 once it ends; the registered BUSY tracks the output enable cycle for cycle at every stretch
* Internal cross-trigger pulse delivered to the CTM destination on an external trigger

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure a seeded CTP via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`: CONFIG.MODE=0 (Wire-OR), CONFIG.INVERT=0, STRETCH_MULT=0x000F (16 clocks).
. Drive a single-cycle internal cross-trigger request to the CTP (via CTM routing).
a. Observe CT_Req_out on the CTP BFM; verify its enable asserts and remains asserted for exactly (STRETCH_MULT{plus}1) clock cycles.
b. Measure the registered BUSY flop alongside the enable: the same width and the same first-rise cycle.
c. Once the enable has risen and STRETCH_MULT >= 8, read CTP STATUS while the pulse is active and verify BUSY=1; after the pulse ends read STATUS and the busy flop and verify both are 0.
. Drive a short CT_Req_in pulse from the CTP BFM (simulate an external trigger).
a. Verify the input is synchronized to `clk_i` and the resulting internal cross-trigger pulse is delivered to the programmed CTM destination.
. Reprogram STRETCH_MULT=0x0000 (1 clock) and a seeded value 1..14, repeat the internal trigger, and verify the output width and the BUSY behavior for each.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctp_wire_or_csr_chk` |CONFIG/STRETCH_MULT writes read back the programmed route configuration
|`ctp_wire_or_stretch_chk` |Output enable width equals (STRETCH_MULT{plus}1) clocks for every multiplier value
|`ctp_wire_or_sync_chk` |External CT_Req_in is synchronized to `clk_i` and delivered to the CTM destination
|`ctp_wire_or_busy_chk` |STATUS.BUSY reads 1 while a pulse of STRETCH_MULT >= 8 is active and 0 after it ends; the registered BUSY has the enable's width (STRETCH_MULT{plus}1) and rises in the same cycle for every stretch
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-CSR` (the CONFIG and STRETCH_MULT readbacks, STATUS.BUSY while the wide pulse is active and after it ends), `CHK-XTRIG-SIGNAL` (the request enable and the busy flop rising together and the synchronized external request delivered), `CHK-XTRIG-STRETCH` (the enable width STRETCH_MULT{plus}1 for the wide, minimum, and seeded multipliers) required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_csr` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-CSR`).

*Pass Criteria*: Wire-OR pulse stretching, input synchronization, and BUSY tracking match the reference model for both stretch settings, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-62-dtp_xtrig_p2p_test]]
===== 6.2 `dtp_xtrig_p2p_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTP/CTM BFMs

*Objective*: CTP point-to-point

*Test Coverage*:

* CTP point-to-point 4-phase handshake (CONFIG.MODE=1) in both directions
* Request path: CT_Req_out asserts on the trigger, drops on CT_Ack_in, and the port idles once CT_Ack_in deasserts; response path: CT_Ack_out asserts on CT_Req_in and drops once it deasserts
* STATUS reflects each handshake phase (BUSY, REQ_OUT, ACK_IN, REQ_IN, ACK_OUT) and STATUS.BUSY tracks the in-flight handshake on both sides
* Internal cross-trigger delivered to the CTM destination on an external trigger

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure a seeded CTP via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`: CONFIG.MODE=1 (Point-to-Point), CONFIG.INVERT=0.
. Drive an internal cross-trigger request to the CTP (via CTM routing).
a. Verify CT_Req_out asserts on the CTP BFM; read STATUS and verify BUSY=1 and REQ_OUT=1.
b. Drive CT_Ack_in from the CTP BFM (simulate the external responder); verify CT_Req_out deasserts; read STATUS and verify ACK_IN=1, REQ_OUT=0, BUSY=1.
c. Deassert CT_Ack_in; wait for the busy flop to clear and read STATUS; verify BUSY=0 and ACK_IN=0.
. Drive CT_Req_in from the CTP BFM (simulate an external trigger).
a. Verify CT_Ack_out asserts and the internal cross-trigger is delivered to the programmed CTM destination; read STATUS and verify REQ_IN=1, ACK_OUT=1, BUSY=1.
b. Deassert CT_Req_in; verify CT_Ack_out deasserts; wait for the busy flop to clear and read STATUS; verify BUSY=0, REQ_IN=0, ACK_OUT=0.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctp_p2p_handshake_chk` |Req/Ack 4-phase handshake completes in both directions at the pad level
|`ctp_p2p_fsm_chk` |STATUS reflects each phase: REQ_OUT=1, then ACK_IN=1 with REQ_OUT=0 on the request side; REQ_IN=1 with ACK_OUT=1 on the response side; every field 0 once the handshake completes
|`ctp_p2p_busy_chk` |STATUS.BUSY is 1 in every in-flight phase of both handshakes and 0 once the acknowledge (request side) or the request (response side) deasserts
|`ctp_p2p_deliver_chk` |External trigger is delivered to the CTM destination
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-CSR` (the CONFIG readback and the five STATUS fields in every handshake phase), `CHK-XTRIG-SIGNAL` (the request and acknowledge pads through both four-phase handshakes) required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_csr` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-CSR`).

*Pass Criteria*: Both handshake directions complete with correct FSM sequencing, BUSY behavior, and trigger delivery, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-63-dtp_xtrig_reset_test]]
===== 6.3 `dtp_xtrig_reset_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTP/CTM BFMs

*Objective*: CTP reset recovery

*Test Coverage*:

* CONFIG.RESET recovery from a P2P handshake deadlock (stalled external ack)
* System reset (`rst_n_i`) landing on an active inverted wire-OR pulse clears all CTP register state back to defaults
* No request, acknowledge, or busy activity while either reset holds or across its release
* Normal operation resumes after recovery (a fresh handshake proceeds)

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure a seeded CTP in P2P mode and initiate a handshake.
a. Stall the external CT_Ack_in on the CTP BFM to simulate a deadlock.
b. Read STATUS; verify BUSY=1 and REQ_OUT=1 (handshake stuck).
. Recover via CONFIG.RESET.
a. Write CONFIG.RESET=1 via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`.
b. Verify the handshake is force-cleared: CT_Req_out deasserts and STATUS reads BUSY=0 and REQ_OUT=0.
c. Write CONFIG.RESET=0 to release; verify CTP returns to normal operation and a new handshake proceeds to completion through the route window.
. Program a second CTP as inverted wire-OR with STRETCH_MULT=0xFFFF, route an internal CT to it, and pulse the source; verify its output enable is active.
. Assert system reset (`rst_n_i`) while that pulse is active and watch every request/acknowledge output and every CTP busy flop from the first held cycle until after release.
a. Verify CONFIG, STRETCH_MULT, and STATUS of every CTP and every `CT_SRC` select read their reset values after release.
b. Verify zero activity on every watched output and busy flop across the reset window.
. Reprogram a post-reset route on the reset CTP and verify it operates correctly.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctp_config_reset_chk` |CONFIG.RESET clears a stalled P2P handshake: CT_Req_out deasserts and STATUS reads BUSY=0, REQ_OUT=0
|`ctp_sys_reset_chk` |System reset returns CONFIG, STRETCH_MULT, and STATUS of all CTPs and every `CT_SRC` select to their reset values
|`ctp_reset_no_spurious_chk` |The reset window (held cycles through release) records zero activity on every request/acknowledge output and CTP busy flop, the active stretched pulse included
|`ctp_reset_recovery_chk` |CTP resumes normal operation: a post-config-reset handshake and a post-system-reset route both match the reference model
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-CSR` (STATUS during the stalled handshake and after CONFIG.RESET, every CTP CONFIG, STRETCH_MULT, and STATUS and every CT_SRC select at its reset value after the system reset), `CHK-XTRIG-SIGNAL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ROUTE-MODEL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ISOLATION` (the recovered handshake and the post-reset route), `CHK-XTRIG-QUIET` (every request and acknowledge output and every busy flop across the reset window) required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_csr` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-CSR`).

*Pass Criteria*: CONFIG.RESET recovers from deadlock, system reset restores defaults with no spurious activity, and post-reset operation matches the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-64-dtp_xtrig_rand_test]]
===== 6.4 `dtp_xtrig_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTP/CTM BFMs

*Objective*: Random CTP configuration

*Test Coverage*:

* Randomized CTP configuration across multiple CTP indices, both modes, and varied settings
* MODE x INVERT x STRETCH_MULT combination coverage
* INVERT flips the pad polarity in both Wire-OR and P2P modes; the bench drives the request and acknowledge pads at the port's polarity
* STRETCH_MULT respected in Wire-OR mode for each randomized value

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. For N iterations:
a. Randomly select a CTP index and an internal CT; the first two iterations take Wire-OR and P2P mode in turn so every pass records both, the rest draw the mode at random.
b. Randomly choose a legal configuration: MODE (0/1), INVERT (0/1), STRETCH_MULT (0..7, so every pulse drains inside the route window).
c. Write CONFIG and STRETCH_MULT via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`; a P2P draw asserts CONFIG.RESET first so no request latched under a previous mode is pending.
d. Route the internal CT to the CTP and pulse it inside an activity window over every output.
e. Verify the window matches the reference model with no unselected output; wire-OR: the enable pulse width is (STRETCH_MULT{plus}1) and the data pad holds the static level INVERT selects; P2P: the request asserts at the port's active level, the acknowledge driven at the port's polarity releases it, and the request returns to its idle level.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctp_rand_csr_chk` |Random CONFIG/STRETCH_MULT writes read back as programmed
|`ctp_rand_mode_chk` |Wire-OR draws: enable width (STRETCH_MULT{plus}1) and a quiet unselected set; P2P draws: request asserted, released by the acknowledge, idle again
|`ctp_rand_invert_chk` |INVERT flips the pad levels: the wire-OR data pad holds the inverted static level while its enable pulses; the P2P request asserts low and idles high with the acknowledge driven low-active
|`ctp_rand_route_chk` |Every draw's output window matches the CTM reference model with no unselected output firing
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-CSR`, `CHK-XTRIG-SIGNAL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ROUTE-MODEL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ISOLATION` (per draw: the CONFIG and STRETCH_MULT readbacks, the request and acknowledge pads at the port's polarity, the fired outputs against the CTM model, no unselected output), `CHK-XTRIG-STRETCH` (the enable width of every wire-OR draw) required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_csr` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-CSR`).

*Pass Criteria*: All randomized configurations produce reference-model-matching behavior, including INVERT polarity and STRETCH_MULT timing, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-65-dtp_xtrig_rand_deterministic_csr_sweep_test]]
===== 6.5 `dtp_xtrig_rand_deterministic_csr_sweep_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTP BFM

*Objective*: CTP CSR deterministic sweep

*Test Coverage*:

* Deterministic walk of every XTRIG/CTP regblock CSR field (coverage-closure of byte-enable and masked-write condition holes)
* Per-byte byte-enable writes that random selection can miss in the CTP generated regblock
* Reserved-bit / legal-mask behavior and aliasing checks across CTP CSRs
* Generated routing state produced by each programmed value

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Bring the XTRIG CSR path to idle after reset and disable all CTP routing.
. Walk every CTP CONFIG and STRETCH_MULT with deterministic values, in seeded order.
a. Write the field patterns, the all-ones word, and the inverted patterns through `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`, comparing the full readback word so reserved bits must read 0.
b. Apply every byte strobe (0x1, 0x2, 0x4, 0x8) with a seeded word over a known base and compare the merged word; a strobed reserved lane leaves the register unchanged.
c. Write all ones to STATUS and read it back unchanged.
. Read the neighbouring CTP's CONFIG and STRETCH_MULT before and after each CTP's sweep and verify they are unchanged (no aliasing).
. After the sweep, program one CTP as wire-OR with a seeded STRETCH_MULT, route an internal CT to it, and verify the route window, the reference model, and the pulse width.
. Disable all CTP routing before test exit.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctp_csr_sweep_byteen_chk` |Each byte strobe (0x1, 0x2, 0x4, 0x8) updates only the strobed lanes of CONFIG and STRETCH_MULT; a strobed reserved lane leaves the register unchanged
|`ctp_csr_sweep_readback_chk` |Every full-word pattern reads back its defined fields with the reserved bits 0
|`ctp_csr_sweep_reserved_chk` |All-ones and inverted patterns leave the reserved bits reading 0; a write to STATUS is ignored
|`ctp_csr_sweep_alias_chk` |The neighbouring CTP's CONFIG and STRETCH_MULT are unchanged by the sweep; a routed pulse after the sweep matches the reference model with width (STRETCH_MULT{plus}1)
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-CSR` (every full-word, inverted, and byte-strobe readback of CONFIG and STRETCH_MULT, the ignored STATUS write, the unchanged neighbor), `CHK-XTRIG-SIGNAL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ROUTE-MODEL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ISOLATION`, `CHK-XTRIG-STRETCH` (the routed pulse after the sweep) required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_csr` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-CSR`).

*Pass Criteria*: Every CTP CSR field accepts and returns deterministic values across all byte-enable patterns with correct reserved-bit and routing behavior and no aliasing, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-66-dtp_xtrig_rand_deterministic_dst_port_sweep_test]]
===== 6.6 `dtp_xtrig_rand_deterministic_dst_port_sweep_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTP/CTM BFMs

*Objective*: Destination-port toggle sweep

*Test Coverage*:

* Deterministic sweep of every CTP destination-port selection (coverage-closure of destination-port toggles random selection can miss)
* Exactly the programmed destination toggles for a single source trigger (one-hot delivery)
* No unintended destination ports toggle

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Disable all CTM/CTP routing and idle the CSR path after reset.
. Sweep each destination port selection deterministically.
a. Program one destination-port mask at a time via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`.
b. Drive a single source trigger via CTM routing.
c. Observe the CTP/CTM BFM outputs and verify exactly the programmed destination toggles while all others remain idle.
d. Pulse the internal CT acknowledge for that destination; every route of the sweep is wire-OR, where the acknowledge is unused, so the pulse is stimulus only.
. Repeat across all configured destination-port selections.
. Compare the full toggle sequence against the reference model and disable routing on exit.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctp_dst_port_sweep_csr_chk` |Destination-port masks read back as programmed
|`ctp_dst_port_sweep_onehot_chk` |Exactly the programmed destination toggles per source trigger
|`ctp_dst_port_sweep_iso_chk` |No unprogrammed destination port toggles
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-CSR`, `CHK-XTRIG-SIGNAL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ROUTE-MODEL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ISOLATION` (per output port: the select readback, the routed pulse, the fired set against the CTM model, every other output quiet) required in both realizations; the internal acknowledge pulse after each internal destination is stimulus only, since every route of the sweep is wire-OR; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_csr` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-CSR`).

*Pass Criteria*: Every destination-port selection toggles exactly its programmed destination with no spurious toggles, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-67-dtp_xtrig_reg_stall_test]]
===== 6.7 `dtp_xtrig_reg_stall_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local XTRIG monitor

*Objective*: XTRIG regblock accepted-access path

*Test Coverage*:

* The upstream AXI-Lite path always presents the XTRIG regblock a ready interface, so the regblock local stall (back-pressure) paths are unreachable in the DTP integration: the crossbar's AW and AR READY-low stall counters (`dtp_tb_if`) do not advance across the accesses
* Confirms XTRIG CSR writes and reads complete without corruption when the register interface is exercised
* No trigger state changes occur while an access is in flight: an activity window over every request and acknowledge enable and every CTP busy flop spans the accesses (the pad levels follow the polarity CSR the accesses write, so they stay out of it)
* A routed pulse after the accesses is the positive control of the observables the quiet records judge

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Open an activity window over every request/acknowledge enable and CTP busy flop, then exercise representative XTRIG CSR writes and reads through `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent` on the accepted-access path.
a. Verify each write/read completes without corruption.
b. Close the window after the last access and verify zero activity across it, then a further quiet window after the accesses.
. Verify the crossbar's AW and AR stall counters did not advance across the accesses while the AWVALID and ARVALID counters did.
. Program one wire-OR route and pulse it: the observables the quiet records judged fire for the routed pulse.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`xtrig_reg_stall_unreachable_chk` |The crossbar's AW and AR READY-low stall counters do not advance across the exercised accesses (`CHK-XTRIG-AXIL`)
|`xtrig_reg_stall_csr_chk` |Accepted XTRIG CSR writes/reads read back without corruption
|`xtrig_reg_stall_no_premature_chk` |No request or acknowledge enable and no CTP busy flop changes while the accesses are in flight (`CHK-XTRIG-QUIET`), and the same observables fire for the routed control pulse afterwards
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-CSR` (the three accepted writes read back), `CHK-XTRIG-QUIET` (no cross-trigger activity across the window spanning the accesses and in the quiet window after them), `CHK-XTRIG-AXIL` (the AWVALID and ARVALID counters advanced, the AW and AR stall counters did not) required in both realizations, with the routed control pulse's `CHK-XTRIG-SIGNAL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ROUTE-MODEL`, and `CHK-XTRIG-ISOLATION`; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_csr` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-CSR`).

*Pass Criteria*: Accepted XTRIG CSR accesses complete uncorrupted and the regblock local stall condition is never observed, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-68-dtp_xtrig_axi_channel_skew_test]]
===== 6.8 `dtp_xtrig_axi_channel_skew_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local XTRIG monitor

*Objective*: Manual AXI-Lite channel skew

*Test Coverage*:

* Manual XTRIG AXI-Lite AW/W/R/B channel skew for ready-low and response-hold coverage
* Write-channel skew: AW and W arrival offset in both orders with master-driven valid/ready timing
* Read-channel skew: AR issued with the slave R response held to exercise response-hold paths
* Protocol completion, response ordering, and final register contents preserved under skew

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the XTRIG AXI-Lite master in manual mode so AW/W/AR/B/R channel timing is driven explicitly.
. Apply write-channel skew.
a. Drive AW before W (and W before AW) with a configurable gap; hold the opposite channel valid-low to create ready-low windows.
b. Verify the write completes with a correct B response and the target CSR holds the written value.
. Apply read-channel skew.
a. Issue AR, then hold the R response by keeping the master RREADY low for several cycles (response-hold).
b. Verify ARVALID/ARADDR remain stable while ARREADY is low and the read returns correct data when released.
. Verify response ordering across the skewed accesses and that final XTRIG register contents match the reference model.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`xtrig_axi_skew_aw_w_chk` |AW/W skew in both orders completes with correct B response
|`xtrig_axi_skew_ready_low_chk` |Channels remain stable through ready-low / response-hold windows
|`xtrig_axi_skew_order_chk` |Response ordering is preserved across skewed accesses
|`xtrig_axi_skew_csr_chk` |Final XTRIG CSR contents match the programmed values
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-AXIL` (the B response of the AW-first and W-first writes, the R response, RVALID and RDATA stable across the RREADY hold, the final read data), `CHK-XTRIG-CSR` (the plain write-read between the skewed writes) required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_csr` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-CSR`).

*Pass Criteria*: All skewed AXI-Lite write and read accesses complete with correct responses, stable channels, preserved ordering, and matching CSR contents, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-69-dtp_xtrig_axi_channel_skew_demux_aw_lock_release_test]]
===== 6.9 `dtp_xtrig_axi_channel_skew_demux_aw_lock_release_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local XTRIG monitor

*Objective*: Demux AW-lock release

*Test Coverage*:

* Two outstanding skewed writes through the shared AXI-Lite master: the second AW is presented while the first W is still pending
* The demux's AW-before-W bookkeeping, mirrored from the crossbar as `xtrig_demux_w_pending` (the W-select queue holds the first AW's port), engages on the first AW and releases when its W passes; the second AW is held with AWREADY low meanwhile, counted by the bench stall counter `xtrig_axil_aw_stall_count` and by the VIP
* The demux AW lock flag, mirrored as `xtrig_demux_aw_lock`, stays clear: it engages only when a master port refuses a fresh AW, and every register block and the error subordinate behind this crossbar accept an AW into an empty slot in the same cycle, so that branch is unreachable from the DTP boundary
* Response ordering across the pair proven by distinguishable response codes (OKAY then DECERR) and by both readbacks

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the XTRIG AXI-Lite master in manual mode and open an activity window over the two demux mirrors.
. Issue an AW-first pair to two seeded CTP CONFIG registers: the first W delayed 4..8 cycles, BREADY deferred 1..4 cycles.
a. Verify `xtrig_demux_w_pending` was 1 during the pair and is 0 at its end, and `xtrig_demux_aw_lock` stayed 0.
b. Verify the second AW stalled (VIP stall cycles > 0) with AWVALID and AWADDR stable, and that the bench AW stall counter advanced by the same count.
c. Verify both B responses are OKAY and both CONFIG readbacks match.
. Issue a W-first pair to a CTM select register and an unmapped word (AW delayed 4..8 cycles).
a. Verify the first B is OKAY and the second DECERR, in issue order, with the same demux-state and stall evidence.
b. Verify the CTM select readback matches.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`xtrig_demux_aw_lock_engage_chk` |The first AW of the pair engages the demux's pending-W state (`xtrig_demux_w_pending`=1) and the second AW is held with AWREADY low until the first W passes (stall cycles > 0, AWVALID/AWADDR stable); the AW lock flag stays 0, that branch being unreachable behind subordinates that accept an AW into an empty slot
|`xtrig_demux_aw_lock_release_chk` |The pending-W state releases once the first W passes (`xtrig_demux_w_pending`=0 at the end of the pair) and the bench AW stall counter equals the VIP's stall count
|`xtrig_demux_order_chk` |With the pair's second write aimed at an unmapped word, the first B is OKAY and the second DECERR, in issue order, and both responses complete
|`xtrig_demux_csr_chk` |Both CTP CONFIG readbacks and the CTM select readback match the programmed values
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-AXIL` (both B responses of each pair in issue order, OKAY then DECERR for the unmapped word, the second AW's stall cycles with AWVALID and AWADDR stable, the bench AW stall counter equal to the VIP count, the readbacks), `CHK-XTRIG-AW-LOCK` (`xtrig_demux_w_pending` engaged during the pair and released after it, `xtrig_demux_aw_lock` clear) required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_csr` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-CSR`).

*Pass Criteria*: The `axi_lite_demux` AW lock engages and releases correctly with preserved ordering and matching CSR contents, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-610-dtp_xtrig_axi_channel_skew_read_decode_backpressure_test]]
===== 6.10 `dtp_xtrig_axi_channel_skew_read_decode_backpressure_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local XTRIG monitor

*Objective*: Read decode backpressure

*Test Coverage*:

* Unmapped XTRIG reads decoded to the crossbar's error subordinate, which answers DECERR with its response data word; the xbar's default-master-port path is disabled in this integration, so every decode error comes from the error subordinate
* Two outstanding reads through the shared AXI-Lite master: the second AR is presented while RREADY is held on the first DECERR, and the demux, which admits one read in flight, holds it with ARREADY low (`axi_lite_demux.ar_valid_stable` / `ar_stable`) until the first response is accepted
* Both unmapped reads return DECERR; no deadlock on the backpressured read path
* Stable ARADDR and read data through the decode-backpressure window

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Idle all manual AXI-Lite master outputs after reset; the XTRIG crossbar address map has no subordinate at the unmapped base (the word after the last CTP window) and above.
. Issue two unmapped reads at seeded offsets as one pair: hold the first DECERR response by keeping RREADY=0 for 4..8 cycles while the second AR is presented.
a. Verify the second AR stalled (VIP AR stall cycles > 0) with ARVALID and ARADDR stable across the stall, and that the bench counter `xtrig_axil_ar_stall_count` advanced by the same count.
b. Verify RVALID/RDATA/RRESP stayed stable across the hold.
. Release RREADY, allow the second AR to complete, and verify both responses are DECERR carrying the error subordinate's data word.
. Verify exactly two AR beats were accepted (ARVALID cycles minus stall cycles).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`xtrig_read_decerr_chk` |Both unmapped reads return DECERR
|`xtrig_read_ar_stable_chk` |The second AR is held with ARREADY low while the first response is held (stall cycles > 0), ARVALID and ARADDR stay stable through the stall, the bench AR stall counter equals the VIP's count, and exactly two AR beats are accepted
|`xtrig_read_default_ar_chk` |Each decode error is answered by the crossbar's error subordinate: the read data is its response word (`0xBADCAB1E`); the xbar default master port is disabled in this integration
|`xtrig_read_no_deadlock_chk` |Both responses complete once RREADY is released, and RVALID/RDATA/RRESP hold stable across the RREADY-low window
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-AXIL` (both unmapped reads answered DECERR with the error subordinate's data word, RVALID, RDATA, and RRESP stable across the RREADY hold, exactly two AR beats accepted), `CHK-XTRIG-AR-STALL` (the second AR held with ARVALID and ARADDR stable and the bench AR stall counter equal to the VIP count) required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_decode` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-DECODE`).

*Pass Criteria*: Both unmapped reads return DECERR with stable channels through the backpressure window and the xbar default-read and demux read-stability assertions covered, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

NOTE: In the CTM entries that follow, `+CT_SRC[dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT+` is
the register of output port `dst`, and each set bit names a source input port
(see the port-naming note at the head of this section).

[[sec-611-dtp_ctm_wire_or_cla_to_ctp_test]]
===== 6.11 `dtp_ctm_wire_or_cla_to_ctp_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: Wire-OR CLA to CTP

*Test Coverage*:

* CTM wire-OR (multicast) routing of one internal CLA source to multiple external CTP destinations
* `+CT_SRC[dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT+` of every selected CTP output programmed with the single CLA source-input bit; the overlap step adds a second source-input bit to one of them
* All selected CTP destinations receive the trigger on the same source pulse; non-selected ports stay idle
* Wire-OR superposition when a second overlapping CLA source targets one of the same CTP destinations
* CSR readback of each selected output register matches its programmed source-input bits

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Program the multicast route via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`:
a. Write `+CT_SRC[dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT = (1 << src)+` for each selected CTP output `dst` (e.g. outputs 0, 5, 12).
b. Read each written register back and confirm only the source-input bit is set.
. Drive a cross-trigger request on the CLA source via the OCAH-local CTM BFM.
. Observe the CTP destinations with the OCAH-local CTP monitor:
a. Verify every selected CTP destination asserts the trigger simultaneously.
b. Verify every non-selected destination port remains quiet.
. Exercise wire-OR superposition:
a. Program one CTP output's `CT_DST_SELECT` with the source-input bit and a second CLA source-input bit.
b. Pulse each source alone, then both in the same cycle: the shared destination fires for each, and the merged pulse has the single-source width (STRETCH_MULT{plus}1, two cycles) while every other destination stays quiet.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctm_wire_or_cla_to_ctp_csr_chk` |CSR readback of each selected output's `CT_DST_SELECT` matches its programmed source-input bits
|`ctm_wire_or_cla_to_ctp_route_chk` |All selected CTP destinations assert on the CLA pulse against the reference model
|`ctm_wire_or_cla_to_ctp_or_chk` |Each of the two sources selected on the shared CTP output fires it alone; both pulsed in the same cycle produce one merged pulse of width (STRETCH_MULT{plus}1)
|`ctm_wire_or_cla_to_ctp_nospur_chk` |No non-selected destination port toggles
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-CSR`, `CHK-XTRIG-SIGNAL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ROUTE-MODEL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ISOLATION` (each selected output's select readback, the routed pulse, the fired outputs against the CTM model for each source alone and for both in the same cycle, no unselected output), `CHK-XTRIG-STRETCH` (the merged pulse width) required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_csr` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-CSR`).

*Pass Criteria*: The CLA source multicasts to exactly its selected CTP destinations, overlapping sources OR correctly, and no spurious destination toggles occur, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-612-dtp_ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_cla_test]]
===== 6.12 `dtp_ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_cla_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: Wire-OR CTP to CLA

*Test Coverage*:

* CTM wire-OR routing of one external CTP source to multiple internal CLA destinations
* `+CT_SRC[dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT+` of every selected CLA output programmed with the single CTP source-input bit
* All selected CLA destinations observe the trigger on the same CTP input pulse; non-selected stay idle
* Wire-OR superposition when an overlapping CTP source targets a shared CLA destination
* CSR readback of each selected output register matches its programmed source-input bits

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Program the multicast route via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`:
a. Write `+CT_SRC[dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT = (1 << src)+` for each selected CLA output `dst`.
b. Read each written register back and confirm only the source-input bit is set.
. Drive a cross-trigger request on the external CTP source via the OCAH-local CTP BFM.
. Observe the CLA destinations with the OCAH-local CTM monitor:
a. Verify every selected CLA destination asserts simultaneously.
b. Verify non-selected CLA destinations remain quiet.
. Exercise wire-OR superposition with a second CTP source selected on one CLA output: pulse each source alone, then both in the same cycle, and confirm the shared CLA destination fires for each and the merged pulse has the single-source width (two cycles, the internal CTP stretch).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_cla_csr_chk` |CSR readback of each selected output's `CT_DST_SELECT` matches its programmed source-input bits
|`ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_cla_route_chk` |All selected CLA destinations assert on the CTP pulse against the reference model
|`ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_cla_or_chk` |Each of the two sources selected on the shared CLA output fires it alone; both pulsed in the same cycle produce one merged two-cycle pulse
|`ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_cla_nospur_chk` |No non-selected CLA destination toggles
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-CSR`, `CHK-XTRIG-SIGNAL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ROUTE-MODEL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ISOLATION` (each selected output's select readback, the routed pulse, the fired outputs against the CTM model for each source alone and for both in the same cycle, no unselected output), `CHK-XTRIG-STRETCH` (the merged pulse width) required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_csr` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-CSR`).

*Pass Criteria*: The CTP source multicasts to exactly its selected CLA destinations, overlapping sources OR correctly, and no spurious destination toggles occur, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-613-dtp_ctm_wire_or_cla_to_cla_test]]
===== 6.13 `dtp_ctm_wire_or_cla_to_cla_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: Wire-OR CLA to CLA

*Test Coverage*:

* CTM wire-OR routing entirely between internal CLA channels (source CLA to multiple destination CLA)
* `+CT_SRC[dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT+` of every selected CLA output programmed with the single CLA source-input bit
* All selected destination CLA channels pulse on the source CLA pulse; non-selected stay idle
* Wire-OR superposition of two CLA sources sharing a destination CLA channel
* No feedback loop where a destination CLA re-triggers its own source route

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Program the intra-CLA multicast route via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`:
a. Write `+CT_SRC[dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT = (1 << src)+` for each selected CLA output `dst`.
b. Read each written register back and confirm only the source-input bit is set.
. Pulse the source CLA via the OCAH-local CTM BFM.
. Observe the destination CLA channels with the OCAH-local CTM monitor:
a. Verify every selected destination CLA pulses.
b. Verify the routing mask behavior: non-selected CLA channels stay idle and the source is not fed back to itself.
. Exercise wire-OR superposition with a second CLA source selected on one destination channel: pulse each source alone, then both in the same cycle, and confirm the shared channel fires for each and the merged pulse has the single-source width (two cycles, the internal CTP stretch).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctm_wire_or_cla_to_cla_csr_chk` |CSR readback of each selected output's `CT_DST_SELECT` matches its programmed source-input bits
|`ctm_wire_or_cla_to_cla_route_chk` |All selected destination CLA channels pulse against the reference model
|`ctm_wire_or_cla_to_cla_or_chk` |Each of the two sources selected on the shared CLA channel fires it alone; both pulsed in the same cycle produce one merged two-cycle pulse
|`ctm_wire_or_cla_to_cla_noloop_chk` |No feedback loop or self-retrigger of the source CLA
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-CSR`, `CHK-XTRIG-SIGNAL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ROUTE-MODEL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ISOLATION` (each selected output's select readback, the routed pulse, the fired outputs against the CTM model for each source alone and for both in the same cycle, no unselected output), `CHK-XTRIG-STRETCH` (the merged pulse width) required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_csr` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-CSR`).

*Pass Criteria*: The CLA source multicasts to exactly its selected CLA destinations with correct wire-OR superposition and no feedback or spurious toggles, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-614-dtp_ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_ctp_test]]
===== 6.14 `dtp_ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_ctp_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: Wire-OR CTP to CTP

*Test Coverage*:

* CTM wire-OR routing entirely between external CTP ports (source CTP to multiple destination CTP)
* `+CT_SRC[dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT+` of every selected CTP output programmed with the single CTP source-input bit
* All selected destination CTP ports pulse with matching pulse width on the source CTP pulse
* Wire-OR superposition of two CTP sources sharing a destination CTP port
* No feedback loop from a destination CTP port back into the source route

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Program the inter-CTP multicast route via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`:
a. Write `+CT_SRC[dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT = (1 << src)+` for each selected CTP output `dst`.
b. Read each written register back and confirm only the source-input bit is set.
. Pulse the source CTP via the OCAH-local CTP BFM.
. Observe the destination CTP ports with the OCAH-local CTP monitor:
a. Verify every selected destination CTP port pulses.
b. Verify pulse width on each destination matches the reference model.
c. Verify no feedback loop re-triggers the source.
. Exercise wire-OR superposition with a second CTP source selected on one destination port: pulse each source alone, then both in the same cycle, and confirm the shared port fires for each and the merged pulse has the single-source width (STRETCH_MULT{plus}1, two cycles).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_ctp_csr_chk` |CSR readback of each selected output's `CT_DST_SELECT` matches its programmed source-input bits
|`ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_ctp_route_chk` |All selected destination CTP ports pulse against the reference model
|`ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_ctp_width_chk` |Each of the two sources selected on the shared CTP port fires it alone; both pulsed in the same cycle produce one merged pulse of width (STRETCH_MULT{plus}1)
|`ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_ctp_noloop_chk` |No feedback loop or self-retrigger of the source CTP
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-CSR`, `CHK-XTRIG-SIGNAL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ROUTE-MODEL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ISOLATION` (each selected output's select readback, the routed pulse, the fired outputs against the CTM model for each source alone and for both in the same cycle, no unselected output), `CHK-XTRIG-STRETCH` (the merged pulse width) required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_csr` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-CSR`).

*Pass Criteria*: The CTP source multicasts to exactly its selected CTP destinations with correct pulse width, wire-OR superposition, and no feedback or spurious toggles, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-615-dtp_ctm_p2p_cla_to_ctp_test]]
===== 6.15 `dtp_ctm_p2p_cla_to_ctp_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: P2P CLA to CTP

*Test Coverage*:

* CTM point-to-point routing of an internal CLA source to a single external CTP destination
* `+CT_SRC[ctp_dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT+` programmed with exactly one source-input bit set
* Request appears only at the selected CTP destination; no spurious triggers elsewhere
* Several distinct CLA-source / CTP-destination pairs exercised to vary the single-bit position

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Program the point-to-point route via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`:
a. Write `+CT_SRC[ctp_dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT = (1 << src)+` so the destination CTP output selects the single source input.
b. Read the register back and confirm only the one source-input bit is set.
. Drive a cross-trigger request on the CLA source via the OCAH-local CTM BFM.
. Observe the destinations with the OCAH-local CTP monitor:
a. Verify the request appears at exactly the selected CTP destination.
b. Verify no other destination port observes the request.
. Repeat for several CLA-source / CTP-destination combinations to move the single set bit across the destination field.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctm_p2p_cla_to_ctp_csr_chk` |CSR readback of the destination CTP output register shows exactly one source-input bit set
|`ctm_p2p_cla_to_ctp_route_chk` |Trigger reaches exactly the selected CTP destination against the reference model
|`ctm_p2p_cla_to_ctp_nospur_chk` |No spurious triggers on non-selected destinations
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-CSR`, `CHK-XTRIG-SIGNAL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ROUTE-MODEL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ISOLATION` (per pair: the single-bit select readback, the routed pulse, exactly the selected destination against the CTM model, every other destination quiet) required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_csr` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-CSR`).

*Pass Criteria*: The CLA-to-CTP trigger is delivered only to the configured destination across all exercised pairs with no spurious activity, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-619-dtp_ctm_reset_wire_or_mode_test]]
===== 6.19 `dtp_ctm_reset_wire_or_mode_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: CTM reset in wire-OR mode

*Test Coverage*:

* System reset (`rst_n_i`) clears all CTM wire-OR routing registers to `0x0000_0000` and every CTP CONFIG, STRETCH_MULT, and STATUS to its reset value
* No request, acknowledge, or busy activity on any destination while the reset holds or across its release
* CTM accepts new wire-OR configuration after reset and routes correctly
* Pre-reset multicast routing and the stretched pulses still active when the reset lands do not survive it

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish pre-reset wire-OR state:
a. Program two CTP outputs with STRETCH_MULT=0xFFFF and select one CLA source on both `+CT_SRC[dst]_CONFIG_0+` registers via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`.
b. Pulse the source and confirm both output enables are active and STATUS.BUSY reads 1.
. Assert system reset (`rst_n_i`) while the stretched pulses are active and watch every request/acknowledge output and every CTP busy flop from the first held cycle until after release.
. Verify clearing and quiescence:
a. Read all `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` and confirm each is `0x0000_0000`; read every CTP CONFIG, STRETCH_MULT, and STATUS and confirm the reset values.
b. Confirm zero activity on every watched output and busy flop across the reset window.
. Reconfigure a fresh wire-OR route to two or three outputs post-reset, pulse the source, and verify correct multicast routing resumes.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctm_reset_wire_or_mode_csr_chk` |All `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` read back `0x0000_0000` and every CTP CONFIG, STRETCH_MULT, and STATUS reads its reset value after reset
|`ctm_reset_wire_or_mode_nospur_chk` |The stretched pulses active when the reset lands are cleared: zero activity on every request/acknowledge output and CTP busy flop while the reset holds and across its release
|`ctm_reset_wire_or_mode_route_chk` |Post-reset wire-OR reconfiguration to two or three outputs routes correctly against the reference model
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-CSR`, `CHK-XTRIG-SIGNAL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ROUTE-MODEL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ISOLATION` (the pre-reset route, every CT_SRC select and every CTP CONFIG, STRETCH_MULT, and STATUS at its reset value, the post-reset route against the CTM model), `CHK-XTRIG-QUIET` (every request and acknowledge output and every busy flop across the reset window) required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_csr` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-CSR`).

*Pass Criteria*: Reset clears all wire-OR routing state with no spurious destination toggles, and post-reset multicast routing matches the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-620-dtp_ctm_reset_p2p_mode_test]]
===== 6.20 `dtp_ctm_reset_p2p_mode_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: CTM reset in P2P mode

*Test Coverage*:

* System reset (`rst_n_i`) clears all CTM point-to-point routing registers to `0x0000_0000` and every CTP CONFIG, STRETCH_MULT, and STATUS to its reset value
* A point-to-point request still awaiting its acknowledge when the reset lands is cleared: CT_Req_out drops and STATUS reads BUSY=0, REQ_OUT=0
* No request, acknowledge, or busy activity on any destination while the reset holds or across its release
* A post-reset point-to-point transaction completes correctly

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish pre-reset P2P state:
a. Program a CTP output in P2P mode and select one CLA source on its `+CT_SRC[dst]_CONFIG_0+` via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`.
b. Pulse the source without driving the acknowledge; confirm CT_Req_out is asserted and STATUS reads BUSY=1, REQ_OUT=1.
. Assert system reset (`rst_n_i`) with the request pending and watch every request/acknowledge output and every CTP busy flop from the first held cycle until after release.
. Verify clearing and quiescence:
a. Read STATUS and confirm BUSY=0 and REQ_OUT=0; read all `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` and confirm each is `0x0000_0000`; read every CTP CONFIG, STRETCH_MULT, and STATUS and confirm the reset values.
b. Confirm zero activity on every watched output and busy flop across the reset window.
. Reprogram a single-bit P2P route post-reset, drive the source, and verify the transaction completes only at the selected destination.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctm_reset_p2p_mode_csr_chk` |All `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` read back `0x0000_0000` and every CTP CONFIG, STRETCH_MULT, and STATUS reads its reset value after reset
|`ctm_reset_p2p_mode_nospur_chk` |The request pending when the reset lands is cleared (CT_Req_out low, STATUS BUSY=0 and REQ_OUT=0 afterwards) with zero activity on every request/acknowledge output and CTP busy flop across the reset window
|`ctm_reset_p2p_mode_route_chk` |Post-reset point-to-point transaction completes at the selected destination against the reference model
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-CSR`, `CHK-XTRIG-SIGNAL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ROUTE-MODEL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ISOLATION` (the pre-reset route, every CT_SRC select and every CTP CONFIG, STRETCH_MULT, and STATUS at its reset value, the post-reset route against the CTM model), `CHK-XTRIG-QUIET` (every request and acknowledge output and every busy flop across the reset window) required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_csr` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-CSR`).

*Pass Criteria*: Reset clears all point-to-point routing and handshake state with no spurious activity, and a post-reset P2P transaction completes correctly, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-621-dtp_ctm_reset_all_modes_test]]
===== 6.21 `dtp_ctm_reset_all_modes_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: CTM reset across all modes

*Test Coverage*:

* System reset (`rst_n_i`) clears CTM routing state programmed across both wire-OR and point-to-point modes
* All `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` return to `0x0000_0000` default regardless of pre-reset mode mix
* No spurious cross-trigger requests on any destination during or after reset
* Both wire-OR and point-to-point routes can be reprogrammed and route correctly post-reset

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish a mixed pre-reset configuration, programmed together so both classes are live when the reset lands:
a. Select one wire-OR source on two CTP outputs and one internal CT output, and one P2P source on a third CTP output, via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`, without clearing between them.
b. Drive each source and confirm correct routing before reset (the internal CT output is a live observable of this scenario).
c. Leave a P2P request pending without its acknowledge.
. Assert system reset (`rst_n_i`) with both route classes programmed and the request pending, and watch every request/acknowledge output and every CTP busy flop from the first held cycle until after release; the pending port reads BUSY=0 and REQ_OUT=0 afterwards.
. Verify clearing and quiescence:
a. Read all `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` and confirm each is `0x0000_0000`; read every CTP CONFIG, STRETCH_MULT, and STATUS and confirm the reset values.
b. Confirm zero activity on every watched output and busy flop across the reset window.
. Reprogram both a wire-OR and a point-to-point route post-reset, pulse the sources, and verify each routes correctly.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctm_reset_all_modes_csr_chk` |All `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` read back `0x0000_0000` and every CTP CONFIG, STRETCH_MULT, and STATUS reads its reset value after reset regardless of pre-reset mode
|`ctm_reset_all_modes_nospur_chk` |Zero activity on every request/acknowledge output and CTP busy flop while the reset holds and across its release
|`ctm_reset_all_modes_route_chk` |Post-reset wire-OR and P2P reconfiguration both route correctly against the reference model
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-CSR`, `CHK-XTRIG-SIGNAL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ROUTE-MODEL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ISOLATION` (the pre-reset route, every CT_SRC select and every CTP CONFIG, STRETCH_MULT, and STATUS at its reset value, the post-reset route against the CTM model), `CHK-XTRIG-QUIET` (every request and acknowledge output and every busy flop across the reset window) required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_csr` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-CSR`).

*Pass Criteria*: Reset returns all routing state to defaults for both modes with no spurious activity, and both wire-OR and P2P routes reprogram correctly, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-622-dtp_ctm_rand_all_scenarios_test]]
===== 6.22 `dtp_ctm_rand_all_scenarios_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: Random CTM all scenarios

*Test Coverage*:

* Randomized routes across all CTM ports spanning both wire-OR and P2P: every selected output's `+CT_SRC[dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT+` holds the source mask of the iteration
* Random source/destination draws across CLA and CTP classes: a wire-OR iteration draws one or two sources and two to four outputs, a P2P iteration one source and one output
* All observed routing matches the CTM reference model for every random configuration
* No routing corruption or state leakage between successive random configurations (every route is programmed over cleared selects)
* Wire-OR superposition: two sources selected on the same outputs and pulsed in the same cycle fire exactly the modelled outputs

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. For N iterations:
a. Draw the mode, one or two sources (wire-OR) or one (P2P), and the outputs; the sources stay out of the output set.
b. Clear every `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+`, write the source mask into each selected output's register via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`, and read each back to confirm it.
c. Pulse the drawn sources in the same cycle via the OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs inside an activity window over every output.
d. Verify the outputs that fired anywhere in the window are exactly the reference model's prediction for the pulsed sources, and that no unselected output fired.
e. Verify every selected output is idle again at the end of the window.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctm_rand_all_scenarios_csr_chk` |Every selected output's `CT_DST_SELECT` reads back the iteration's source mask
|`ctm_rand_all_scenarios_route_chk` |All observed routing matches the reference model across iterations
|`ctm_rand_all_scenarios_or_chk` |Two sources selected on the same outputs and pulsed in the same cycle fire exactly the modelled outputs (one merged pulse each) with no unselected output
|`ctm_rand_all_scenarios_noleak_chk` |No unselected output fires and every selected output is idle at the end of each iteration's window
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-CSR`, `CHK-XTRIG-SIGNAL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ROUTE-MODEL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ISOLATION` (per iteration: every selected output's select readback, the routed pulses, the fired set against the CTM model, no unselected output and every selected output idle at the window's end) required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_csr` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-CSR`).

*Pass Criteria*: Every random mixed-mode configuration routes exactly as modeled with correct wire-OR superposition and no inter-configuration leakage, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-623-dtp_ctm_rand_wire_or_only_test]]
===== 6.23 `dtp_ctm_rand_wire_or_only_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: Random CTM wire-OR only

*Test Coverage*:

* Randomized wire-OR (multicast) routing only: every iteration draws one or two sources and two to four outputs, and each selected output's `+CT_SRC[dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT+` holds the source mask (a multi-bit register is a multi-source OR onto that output)
* Random source pulses fan out to all selected destinations simultaneously
* Wire-OR superposition: two sources pulsed in the same cycle fire exactly the outputs that select them
* Non-selected destinations remain quiet for every random configuration

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. For N iterations:
a. Draw one or two sources and two to four outputs (sources outside the output set).
b. Clear every `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+`, write the source mask into each selected output's register via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`, and read each back.
c. Pulse the drawn sources in the same cycle via the OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs inside an activity window over every output.
d. Verify the outputs that fired are exactly the reference model's prediction and that every selected destination asserted.
e. Verify non-selected destinations stayed idle and the selected ones are idle again at the end of the window.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctm_rand_wire_or_only_csr_chk` |Every selected output's `CT_DST_SELECT` reads back the iteration's source mask
|`ctm_rand_wire_or_only_route_chk` |All selected destinations assert per the reference model across iterations
|`ctm_rand_wire_or_only_or_chk` |Two sources selected on the same outputs and pulsed in the same cycle fire exactly the modelled outputs
|`ctm_rand_wire_or_only_nospur_chk` |No non-selected destination toggles
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-CSR`, `CHK-XTRIG-SIGNAL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ROUTE-MODEL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ISOLATION` (per iteration: every selected output's select readback, the routed pulses, the fired set against the CTM model, no unselected output and every selected output idle at the window's end) required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_csr` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-CSR`).

*Pass Criteria*: Every random wire-OR configuration multicasts correctly with correct superposition and no spurious destination toggles, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-624-dtp_ctm_rand_p2p_only_test]]
===== 6.24 `dtp_ctm_rand_p2p_only_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: Random CTM P2P only

*Test Coverage*:

* Randomized single-bit `CT_DST_SELECT` masks restricted to point-to-point routing only
* Random request/acknowledge handshakes complete at exactly the selected destination
* No cross-route leakage between independent point-to-point routes: each iteration programs a second, disjoint route alongside the drawn one and pulses each alone
* Coverage of diverse single-bit destination positions across sources

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. For N iterations:
a. Generate random `CT_DST_SELECT` values constrained to exactly one set bit per active source (P2P-only).
b. Write all active `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` registers via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent` and read each back to confirm a single destination bit is set.
c. Drive randomized handshakes on random source ports via the OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs.
d. Verify each request/ack completes only at its configured single destination.
e. Program a second point-to-point route on a disjoint source and destination without clearing the first, pulse each source alone, and verify the other route's destination stays quiet (no cross-route leakage).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctm_rand_p2p_only_csr_chk` |Every randomized P2P mask reads back with exactly one destination bit set
|`ctm_rand_p2p_only_route_chk` |Each request/ack completes at the selected destination per the reference model
|`ctm_rand_p2p_only_noleak_chk` |No cross-route leakage between independent point-to-point routes
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-CSR`, `CHK-XTRIG-SIGNAL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ROUTE-MODEL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ISOLATION` (per iteration: every selected output's select readback, the routed pulses, the fired set against the CTM model, no unselected output and every selected output idle at the window's end) required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_csr` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-CSR`).

*Pass Criteria*: Every random point-to-point configuration completes its handshake at exactly the configured destination with no cross-route leakage, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-625-dtp_ctm_rand_cla_to_ctp_test]]
===== 6.25 `dtp_ctm_rand_cla_to_ctp_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: Random CTM CLA to CTP

*Test Coverage*:

* Randomized CTM routes restricted to internal CLA sources targeting external CTP destinations
* Random single-bit and multi-bit `CT_DST_SELECT` masks and random trigger timing within the CLA-to-CTP class
* Selected CTP destinations match the random configuration; non-selected stay quiet
* Coverage of diverse CLA-source x CTP-destination route combinations

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. For N iterations:
a. Randomize `CT_DST_SELECT` masks for CLA sources, with destination bits restricted to CTP ports.
b. Write the active `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` registers via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent` and read each back.
c. Drive CLA triggers via the OCAH-local CTM BFM with randomized timing.
d. Observe CTP destinations via the OCAH-local CTP monitor and verify the selected CTP ports respond.
e. Sample the source x destination coverage of the exercised CLA-to-CTP combinations.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctm_rand_cla_to_ctp_csr_chk` |Every randomized CLA-to-CTP mask reads back as programmed
|`ctm_rand_cla_to_ctp_route_chk` |Selected CTP destinations respond per the reference model across iterations
|`ctm_rand_cla_to_ctp_nospur_chk` |No non-selected CTP destination toggles
|===

*Evidence*: `CHK-XTRIG-CSR`, `CHK-XTRIG-SIGNAL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ROUTE-MODEL`, `CHK-XTRIG-ISOLATION` (per iteration: every selected output's select readback, the routed pulses, the fired set against the CTM model, no unselected output and every selected output idle at the window's end) required in both realizations; SV-UVM adds the `xtrig_csr` scoreboard feature (`CHK-SB-XTRIG-CSR`).

*Pass Criteria*: Every random CLA-to-CTP configuration routes correctly to its selected CTP destinations with no spurious activity and full route-combination coverage, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-626-dtp_ctm_rand_ctp_to_cla_test]]
===== 6.26 `dtp_ctm_rand_ctp_to_cla_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: Random CTM CTP to CLA

*Test Coverage*:

* Randomized CTM routes restricted to external CTP sources targeting internal CLA destinations
* Random single-bit and multi-bit `CT_DST_SELECT` masks and random trigger timing within the CTP-to-CLA class
* Selected CLA destinations match the random configuration; non-selected stay quiet
* Coverage of diverse CTP-source x CLA-destination route combinations

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. For N iterations:
a. Randomize `CT_DST_SELECT` masks for CTP sources, with destination bits restricted to CLA channels.
